                "commit_list": u.CommitList, "file_list": u.FileList, "doc_view": u.DocView,
        }
        for _, pg := range merged.pages() {
                for _, r := range fromFile[pg.name] {
                        switch {
                        case !slices.ContainsFunc(*pg.rows, func(b Row) bool { return b.Action == r.Action }):
                                problems = append(problems, "no action "+r.Action+" in "+pg.name)
                        case len(r.Keys) == 0:
                                problems = append(problems, r.Action+" in "+pg.name+" has no keys")
                        case slices.Contains(twoWay, r.Action) && len(r.Keys) != 2:
                                problems = append(problems, r.Action+" in "+pg.name+" takes two keys, one each way")
                        }
                }
                *pg.rows = replaceKeys(*pg.rows, fromFile[pg.name])
        }
        problems = append(problems, merged.conflicts()...)
        if len(problems) > 0 {
                return km, problems
        }
        return merged, nil
}

// replaceKeys returns rows with the rows of each action of user in place of
// its rows: the first where the first was, with the words of the row it
// replaces; rows of user beyond them have none.
func replaceKeys(rows []Row, user []userRow) []Row {
        var out []Row
        done := map[string]bool{}
        for _, r := range rows {
                if !slices.ContainsFunc(user, func(u userRow) bool { return u.Action == r.Action }) {
                        out = append(out, r)
                        continue
                }
                if done[r.Action] {
                        continue
                }
                done[r.Action] = true
                var old []Row
                for _, o := range rows {
                        if o.Action == r.Action {
                                old = append(old, o)
                        }
                }
                i := 0
                for _, u := range user {
                        if u.Action != r.Action {
                                continue
                        }
                        row := Row{Action: u.Action}
                        if i < len(old) {
                                row = old[i]
                        }
                        row.Keys = u.Keys
                        out = append(out, row)
                        i++
                }
        }
        return out
}

// FileName is the user's keybindings.toml in the configuration directory.
const FileName = "keybindings.toml"

// Load returns the built-in keys with those of the user's keybindings.toml in
// the configuration directory dir, if there is one, and what is wrong with
// it; with a problem, the built-in keys.
func Load(dir string) (*Map, []string) {
        data, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, FileName))
        if errors.Is(err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
                return Builtin(), nil
        }
        if err != nil {
                return Builtin(), []string{err.Error()}
        }
        p, problems := Builtin().ApplyUserKeys(string(data))
        if len(problems) > 0 {
                return Builtin(), problems
        }
        return &p, nil
}

// Page is a page whose keys are looked up in keybindings.toml.
type Page int

const (
        CommitList Page = iota
        FileList
        DiffView
        FlowView
        DocView
        Everywhere // the popups over all pages
)

// Hit is what a key does: the action of its row, "" for none, and its index
// in the row, which gives the way of actions that go two ways (see
// keybindings.toml).
type Hit struct {
        Action string
        Index  int
}

// tables returns the rows the keys of page pg are looked up in, in order.
func (km Map) tables(pg Page) [][]Row {
        switch pg {
        case CommitList:
                return [][]Row{km.CommitList, km.Everywhere}
        case FileList:
                return [][]Row{km.FileList, km.Everywhere}
        case DiffView:
                return [][]Row{km.DiffView, km.Everywhere}
        case FlowView:
                return [][]Row{km.FlowView, km.DiffView, km.Everywhere}
        case DocView:
                return [][]Row{km.DocView, km.Everywhere}
        }
        return [][]Row{km.Everywhere}
}

// Key returns what key k, as Bubble Tea names it, does on page pg.
func (km Map) Key(pg Page, k string) Hit {
        for _, rows := range km.tables(pg) {
                for _, r := range rows {
                        for i, name := range r.Keys {
                                if Name(name) == k {
                                        return Hit{r.Action, i}
                                }
                        }
                }
        }
        return Hit{}
}

// Name returns Bubble Tea's name of the key written as name in
// keybindings.toml, which writes pgdn and F5 as the help shows them, and
// <letter> for the letter that follows.
func Name(name string) string {
        name = strings.TrimSuffix(name, "<letter>")
        if name == "pgdn" {
                return "pgdown"
        }
        if n, ok := strings.CutPrefix(name, "F"); ok && n != "" && strings.Trim(n, "0123456789") == "" {
                return "f" + n
        }
        return name
}

// LabelIn returns the word of the status bar in state, "" when there is none.
func (r Row) LabelIn(state int) string {
        if state < len(r.Label) {
                return r.Label[state]
        }
        return ""
}

// Order returns the first key of each row of everywhere, which decides the
// order of the settings.
func (km Map) Order() []string {
        var keys []string
        for _, r := range km.Everywhere {
                if len(r.Keys) > 0 {
                        keys = append(keys, r.Keys[0])
                }
        }
        return keys
}

// Problems returns what is wrong with km by itself: a key with two actions on
// one page, a line of the help with an action that has no help, a placeholder
// without its row. What the settings and the help need of it is checked by
// the package ui.
func (km Map) Problems() []string {
        problems := km.conflicts()
        for _, r := range km.Help {
                if r.Action != "" && km.Row(r.Action).Help == "" {
                        problems = append(problems, "the help's action "+r.Action+" has no help")
                }
        }
        var words []string
        for _, pg := range km.pages() {
                for _, r := range *pg.rows {
                        words = append(words, r.Help, r.Setting)
                }
        }
        for _, r := range km.Help {
                words = append(words, r.Note)
        }
        for _, w := range words {
                for _, m := range placeholder.FindAllStringSubmatch(w, -1) {
                        n, _ := strconv.Atoi(m[2])
                        if n >= len(km.RowsOf(m[1])) {
                                problems = append(problems, "placeholder "+m[0]+" has no row")
                        }
                }
        }
        return problems
}

// conflicts returns the keys with two actions on one page.
func (km Map) conflicts() []string {
        var problems []string
        for _, pg := range km.pages() {
                actions := map[string]string{}
                for _, r := range *pg.rows {
                        for _, name := range r.Keys {
                                if a, ok := actions[Name(name)]; ok && a != r.Action {
                                        problems = append(problems, "key "+name+" has two actions in "+pg.name+": "+a+", "+r.Action)
                                }
                                actions[Name(name)] = r.Action
                        }
                }
        }
        return problems
}

// Package settings holds jdiff's user settings.
package settings

// Settings are the user-configurable options, read from main.toml and,
// for the toggles, state.toml (see package config); the TOML keys are given
// in brackets.
type Settings struct {
        // Context (context) is the number of unchanged lines kept visible around changes
        // while unchanged code is collapsed.
        Context int `toml:"context"`
        // CollapseUnchanged (collapse_unchanged) starts the diff view with
        // unchanged code collapsed.
        CollapseUnchanged bool `toml:"collapse_unchanged"`
        // OnlyChangedWhitespaces (only_changed_whitespaces) compares indentation
        // character by character, and a run of whitespace in place of another
        // one by its length, so only the added or removed part is marked.
        OnlyChangedWhitespaces bool `toml:"only_changed_whitespaces"`
        // WatchInterval (watch_interval) is how often, in seconds, files on disk
        // that are not watched (InotifyMaxDirs) are checked for changes and
        // reloaded while auto reload is on: both files of a comparison, or the
        // current file of the file list and its neighbors. 0 turns the checks
        // off, and watching too.
        WatchInterval float64 `toml:"watch_interval"`
        // AutoReloadInterval (auto_reload_interval) is how often, in seconds, auto
        // reload runs git status, which sees new files, and all changes where
        // Linux does not report them at once.
        AutoReloadInterval int `toml:"auto_reload_interval"`
        // LineEnd (line_end) is drawn at the end of lines while all whitespace
        // is shown; it must be one column wide.
        LineEnd string `toml:"line_end"`
        // CREnd (cr_end) is drawn for the \r of a \r\n line end where it
        // changed, and on all lines while ShowCR is set; one column wide.
        CREnd string `toml:"cr_end"`
        // Extends (extends) and Precedes (precedes) mark lines cut at the edge
        // of their pane while lines are not wrapped: Extends in the last column
        // when the line goes on, Precedes in the first when text is scrolled out
        // to the left; one column wide each.
        Extends  string `toml:"extends"`
        Precedes string `toml:"precedes"`
        // Untracked (untracked) also shows the untracked files in jdiff diff,
        // as added, where it compares with the working tree. The key of action
        // untracked toggles it and --untracked turns it on, for the run only: it
        // is the one toggle state.toml does not keep.
        Untracked bool `toml:"untracked"`
        Toggles
}

// Toggles are the settings switched by keys, which state.toml keeps as last
// set; their TOML keys are on the same level as the other settings. The keys
// are those of the action named with each, in keybindings.toml of package
// keys, which the user may change.
type Toggles struct {
        // Flow (flow) shows all files one after the other instead of one file
        // at a time; action flow.
        Flow bool `toml:"flow"`
        // SideBySide (side_by_side) shows the old and new file next to each
        // other; off, they are shown in one column like git diff; action layout.
        SideBySide bool `toml:"side_by_side"`
        // Preview (preview) shows a box below the commit and file lists with
        // the selected commit's message or file's diff; action preview.
        Preview bool `toml:"preview"`
        // ShowWhitespaces (show_whitespaces) draws all whitespace as · and →,
        // not only changed whitespace, and the end of lines as LineEnd; action
        // whitespace.
        ShowWhitespaces bool `toml:"show_whitespaces"`
        // MarkWhitespace (mark_whitespace) draws changed whitespace as · and →
        // in the style of changed words: indentation, tabs and runs of spaces,
        // not a single space between changed words; off, all changed whitespace
        // looks like unchanged whitespace; action mark_whitespace.
        MarkWhitespace bool `toml:"mark_whitespace"`
        // ShowTrailing (show_trailing) draws whitespace at the end of lines as ·
        // and → in the trailing whitespace style; action trailing.
        ShowTrailing bool `toml:"show_trailing"`
        // ShowCR (show_cr) draws the \r of all \r\n line ends as CREnd, not
        // only of changed ones; action line_ends.
        ShowCR bool `toml:"show_cr"`
        // Wrap (wrap) wraps lines longer than their pane onto further screen
        // lines; off, they are cut and scrolled sideways; action wrap.
        Wrap bool `toml:"wrap"`
        // BoldChanges (bold_changes) draws changed words and whitespace bold
        // where the theme says so; off, without bold, which terminals often
        // draw brighter; action bold.
        BoldChanges bool `toml:"bold_changes"`
        // FullMessages (full_messages) shows the whole commit message in the
        // flow view of the log; off, only the subject; action full_messages.
        FullMessages bool `toml:"full_messages"`
        // StayInCommit (stay_in_commit) keeps the cursor of the flow view of the
        // log in the current commit: moves out of it stop with a warning, the keys
        // of action history still switch commits; action stay_in_commit.
        StayInCommit bool `toml:"stay_in_commit"`
        // SearchRegex (search_regex) searches, action search, for a regular expression;
        // off, for plain text. shift+tab in the search bar toggles it.
        SearchRegex bool `toml:"search_regex"`
        // SearchAll (search_all) searches, action search, in all lines; off, only in
        // changed ones, added, removed and moved. tab in the search bar toggles
        // it.
        SearchAll bool `toml:"search_all"`
        // AutoReloadTimeout (auto_reload_timeout) is after how many minutes
        // without a key pressed or a change of the repository auto reload ends,
        // and with it its git commands; 0 is never. The settings popup sets it.
        AutoReloadTimeout int `toml:"auto_reload_timeout"`
        // InotifyMaxDirs (inotify_max_dirs) is the most directories whose
        // changes the system reports (on Linux, inotify) while auto reload is on,
        // the most recently used ones: the directories of the files on disk, not
        // their subdirectories, and those of the working tree; HEAD and the index
        // are watched besides. Files in the others are checked every
        // WatchInterval. 0 watches only HEAD and the index. The settings popup
        // sets it.
        InotifyMaxDirs int `toml:"inotify_max_dirs"`
}

// Default returns the default settings.
func Default() Settings {
        return Settings{
                Context:                3,
                CollapseUnchanged:      true,
                OnlyChangedWhitespaces: true,
                WatchInterval:          1,
                AutoReloadInterval:     10,
                LineEnd:                "¶",
                CREnd:                  "␍",
                Extends:                "»",
                Precedes:               "«",
                Toggles: Toggles{
                        SideBySide:      true,
                        Preview:         true,
                        ShowWhitespaces: false,
                        MarkWhitespace:  true,
                        BoldChanges:     true,
                        FullMessages:    true,

                        AutoReloadTimeout: 5,
                        InotifyMaxDirs:    100,
                },
        }
}

// Package source loads the files to compare from disk.
package source

import (
        "bytes"
        "errors"
        "io/fs"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "slices"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

// ErrBinary is returned for files that contain NUL bytes.
var ErrBinary = errors.New("binary file")

// File is one file of a comparison.
type File struct {
        Path     string // relative to both sides, slash separated
        OldPath  string // the old path of a renamed file, else empty
        Old, New []string
        // InOld and InNew report whether the file exists on that side.
        InOld, InNew bool
        Binary       bool
        // OldFile and NewFile are the paths of the sides on disk, empty for
        // files from git; OldStamp and NewStamp identify the versions read.
        OldFile, NewFile   string
        OldStamp, NewStamp Stamp
}

// Stamp identifies a version of a file on disk.
type Stamp struct {
        ModTime time.Time
        Size    int64
}

// StampOf returns the stamp of the file at path, following symlinks.
func StampOf(path string) (Stamp, error) {
        info, err := os.Stat(path)
        if err != nil {
                return Stamp{}, err
        }
        return Stamp{info.ModTime(), info.Size()}, nil
}

// PathInOld returns the path of the file on the old side.
func (f File) PathInOld() string {
        if f.OldPath != "" {
                return f.OldPath
        }
        return f.Path
}

// Read reads a text file into lines without their newlines, and returns the
// stamp of the version read; the \r of a \r\n line end is kept, so that
// changed line ends show. The stamp is taken first, so a change while
// reading shows later.
func Read(path string) ([]string, Stamp, error) {
        st, err := StampOf(path)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, Stamp{}, err
        }
        data, err := os.ReadFile(path)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, Stamp{}, err
        }
        lines, err := SplitLines(data)
        return lines, st, err
}

// SplitLines splits text into lines without their newlines, keeping the \r
// of \r\n line ends, or returns ErrBinary for data containing NUL bytes.
func SplitLines(data []byte) ([]string, error) {
        if bytes.IndexByte(data, 0) >= 0 {
                return nil, ErrBinary
        }
        if len(data) == 0 {
                return nil, nil
        }
        return strings.Split(strings.TrimSuffix(string(data), "\n"), "\n"), nil
}

// LoadDirs compares the regular files of two directory trees, as handed over
// by git difftool --dir-diff. Files with identical content are left out.
// Symlinks are followed, since git links work tree files into the new tree.
func LoadDirs(oldDir, newDir string) ([]File, error) {
        oldFiles, err := listFiles(oldDir)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        newFiles, err := listFiles(newDir)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        paths := append(slices.Clone(oldFiles), newFiles...)
        slices.Sort(paths)
        paths = slices.Compact(paths)
        inOld, inNew := pathSet(oldFiles), pathSet(newFiles)

        var out []File
        for _, p := range paths {
                f := File{Path: p, InOld: inOld[p], InNew: inNew[p]}
                if f.InOld {
                        f.OldFile = filepath.Join(oldDir, p)
                        if f.Old, f.OldStamp, err = Read(f.OldFile); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrBinary) {
                                return nil, err
                        }
                        f.Binary = f.Binary || errors.Is(err, ErrBinary)
                }
                if f.InNew {
                        f.NewFile = filepath.Join(newDir, p)
                        if f.New, f.NewStamp, err = Read(f.NewFile); err != nil && !errors.Is(err, ErrBinary) {
                                return nil, err
                        }
                        f.Binary = f.Binary || errors.Is(err, ErrBinary)
                }
                if !f.Binary && f.InOld == f.InNew && slices.Equal(f.Old, f.New) {
                        continue
                }
                out = append(out, f)
        }
        return out, nil
}

func pathSet(paths []string) map[string]bool {
        set := make(map[string]bool, len(paths))
        for _, p := range paths {
                set[p] = true
        }
        return set
}

// listFiles returns the sorted slash-separated paths of all regular files
// below dir, following symlinks to files.
func listFiles(dir string) ([]string, error) {
        var out []string
        err := filepath.WalkDir(dir, func(path string, d fs.DirEntry, err error) error {
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if d.IsDir() {
                        return nil
                }
                info, err := os.Stat(path)
                if err != nil || !info.Mode().IsRegular() {
                        return nil
                }
                rel, err := filepath.Rel(dir, path)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                out = append(out, filepath.ToSlash(rel))
                return nil
        })
        return out, err
}

package theme

import (
        "bytes"
        "os/exec"
        "slices"
        "strings"
)

// Config holds git config entries as printed by git config --get-regexp:
// section and key lowercased, subsections (feature names) as written.
type Config map[string]string

// ReadGitConfig reads the delta and jdiff sections of the effective git
// config, so includes and repository config apply as they do for delta.
// Without git or any matching entries the result is empty.
func ReadGitConfig() Config {
        out, err := exec.Command("git", "config", "--null", "--get-regexp", `^(delta|jdiff)\.`).Output()
        if err != nil {
                return Config{}
        }
        return parseGitConfig(out)
}

// parseGitConfig parses the output of git config --null --get-regexp: one
// "key\nvalue" record per NUL. Later entries win, as in git.
func parseGitConfig(out []byte) Config {
        cfg := Config{}
        for rec := range bytes.SplitSeq(out, []byte{0}) {
                if len(rec) == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                key, value, _ := strings.Cut(string(rec), "\n")
                cfg[key] = value
        }
        return cfg
}

// Features returns the enabled delta features, highest priority first,
// following delta's rules: features from DELTA_FEATURES (env) and --features
// (flag) come first; [delta] features is used only when neither replaces it
// (a leading "+" in env adds instead). Each feature precedes the features it
// enables itself, and among the features of one setting the rightmost wins.
func Features(cfg Config, env, flag string) []string {
        var input []string
        useConfig := flag == ""
        switch {
        case strings.HasPrefix(env, "+"):
                input = append(strings.Fields(env[1:]), strings.Fields(flag)...)
        case env != "":
                input = strings.Fields(env)
                useConfig = false
        default:
                input = strings.Fields(flag)
        }

        var out []string
        var visit func(f string)
        visit = func(f string) {
                out = append(out, f)
                children := strings.Fields(cfg["delta."+f+".features"])
                for _, c := range slices.Backward(children) {
                        if !slices.Contains(out, c) {
                                visit(c)
                        }
                }
        }
        for _, f := range slices.Backward(input) {
                visit(f)
        }
        if useConfig {
                for _, f := range slices.Backward(strings.Fields(cfg["delta.features"])) {
                        visit(f)
                }
        }
        return out
}

// lookup returns the value of a delta option: the main [delta] section wins,
// then the features in priority order.
func lookup(cfg Config, features []string, key string) (string, bool) {
        if v, ok := cfg["delta."+key]; ok {
                return v, true
        }
        for _, f := range features {
                if v, ok := cfg["delta."+f+"."+key]; ok {
                        return v, true
                }
        }
        return "", false
}

package theme

import (
        "path/filepath"
        "reflect"
        "strings"

        "github.com/BurntSushi/toml"
)

// Kind is where a theme comes from.
type Kind int

const (
        KindBuiltin Kind = iota // jdiff's defaults
        KindGit                 // delta's and jdiff's git config sections
        KindFile                // a theme file
)

// Spec is a theme as its source defines it: the values as written, nil for
// options the source leaves unset. Resolve turns it into a Theme.
//
// The TOML keys are the option names; in the git config they are keys of
// the [delta] section, or of [jdiff] where the git tag says so.
type Spec struct {
        Name string `toml:"-"`
        Kind Kind   `toml:"-"`

        MinusStyle            *string `toml:"minus-style"`
        MinusEmphStyle        *string `toml:"minus-emph-style"`
        MinusNonEmphStyle     *string `toml:"minus-non-emph-style"` // unset: minus-style
        PlusStyle             *string `toml:"plus-style"`
        PlusEmphStyle         *string `toml:"plus-emph-style"`
        PlusNonEmphStyle      *string `toml:"plus-non-emph-style"` // unset: plus-style
        ZeroStyle             *string `toml:"zero-style"`
        LineNumbersMinusStyle *string `toml:"line-numbers-minus-style"`
        LineNumbersPlusStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-plus-style"`
        LineNumbersZeroStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-zero-style"`
        LineNumbersLeftStyle  *string `toml:"line-numbers-left-style"`
        LineNumbersRightStyle *string `toml:"line-numbers-right-style"`
        FileStyle             *string `toml:"file-style"`
        FileDecorationStyle   *string `toml:"file-decoration-style"` // only its color is used
        InlineHintStyle       *string `toml:"inline-hint-style"`
        FileAddedLabel        *string `toml:"file-added-label"`
        FileRemovedLabel      *string `toml:"file-removed-label"`
        FileModifiedLabel     *string `toml:"file-modified-label"`
        FileRenamedLabel      *string `toml:"file-renamed-label"`

        AnchorStyle             *string `toml:"anchor-style" git:"jdiff"`
        PendingStyle            *string `toml:"pending-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CursorStyle             *string `toml:"cursor-style" git:"jdiff"`
        FileBarStyle            *string `toml:"file-bar-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CommitBarStyle          *string `toml:"commit-bar-style" git:"jdiff"`
        CommitHashStyle         *string `toml:"commit-hash-style" git:"jdiff"`
        BranchStyle             *string `toml:"branch-style" git:"jdiff"`
        FillerStyle             *string `toml:"filler-style" git:"jdiff"`              // unset: file-decoration-style's color
        WhitespaceStyle         *string `toml:"whitespace-style" git:"jdiff"`          // unset: file-decoration-style's color
        TrailingWhitespaceStyle *string `toml:"trailing-whitespace-style" git:"jdiff"` // shown by t
        HiddenCharStyle         *string `toml:"hidden-char-style" git:"jdiff"`         // ☠ for invisible characters
        OverflowStyle           *string `toml:"overflow-style" git:"jdiff"`            // « » at the edges of cut lines
        MoreStyle               *string `toml:"more-style" git:"jdiff"`                // ↑ ↓ for items of lists out of view; unset: whitespace-style
        SearchStyle             *string `toml:"search-style" git:"jdiff"`              // text found by /
        MinusIndentStyle        *string `toml:"minus-indent-style" git:"jdiff"`        // unset: minus-emph-style
        PlusIndentStyle         *string `toml:"plus-indent-style" git:"jdiff"`         // unset: plus-emph-style
        MinusMovedStyle         *string `toml:"minus-moved-style" git:"jdiff"`         // removed lines that moved elsewhere
        PlusMovedStyle          *string `toml:"plus-moved-style" git:"jdiff"`          // added lines that moved here
        DocHeadingStyle         *string `toml:"doc-heading-style" git:"jdiff"`         // headings of --readme; unset: branch-style
        DocCodeStyle            *string `toml:"doc-code-style" git:"jdiff"`            // code in --readme; unset: file-style
        DocLinkStyle            *string `toml:"doc-link-style" git:"jdiff"`            // links in --readme
        HeadingStyle            *string `toml:"heading-style" git:"jdiff"`             // headings of lists and boxes, the active pane's title
        InactiveHeadingStyle    *string `toml:"inactive-heading-style" git:"jdiff"`    // the inactive pane's title, side by side
        KeyStyle                *string `toml:"key-style" git:"jdiff"`                 // the keys in the help

        // Colors are named colors that styles can use like color names.
        Colors map[string]string `toml:"colors"`
}

// options calls f with the key, git config section and field of each option
// of s.
func (s *Spec) options(f func(key, section string, field **string)) {
        v := reflect.ValueOf(s).Elem()
        for i := range v.NumField() {
                key := v.Type().Field(i).Tag.Get("toml")
                if key == "-" || key == "colors" {
                        continue
                }
                section := v.Type().Field(i).Tag.Get("git")
                if section == "" {
                        section = "delta"
                }
                f(key, section, v.Field(i).Addr().Interface().(**string))
        }
}

// isJdiffKey reports whether key is a [jdiff] key rather than a delta one.
func isJdiffKey(key string) bool {
        jdiff := false
        (&Spec{}).options(func(k, section string, _ **string) {
                jdiff = jdiff || (k == key && section == "jdiff")
        })
        return jdiff
}

// Builtin returns jdiff's default theme. Its colors are made for 16 color
// mode: unlike delta's defaults they are foreground colors only, since ANSI
// backgrounds are too loud for whole lines.
func Builtin() Spec {
        str := func(s string) *string { return &s }
        return Spec{
                Name: "default",
                Kind: KindBuiltin,

                MinusStyle:            str("red"),
                MinusEmphStyle:        str("bright-red bold"),
                PlusStyle:             str("green"),
                PlusEmphStyle:         str("bright-green bold"),
                ZeroStyle:             str("normal"),
                LineNumbersMinusStyle: str("red"),
                LineNumbersPlusStyle:  str("green"),
                LineNumbersZeroStyle:  str("normal"),
                LineNumbersLeftStyle:  str("blue"),
                LineNumbersRightStyle: str("blue"),
                FileStyle:             str("blue"),
                FileDecorationStyle:   str("blue ul"),
                InlineHintStyle:       str("blue"),
                FileAddedLabel:        str("added:"),
                FileRemovedLabel:      str("removed:"),
                FileModifiedLabel:     str(""),
                FileRenamedLabel:      str("renamed:"),

                AnchorStyle:  str("yellow bold"),
                PendingStyle: str("magenta bold"),
                CursorStyle:  str("reverse bold"),
                // Bars with a background, so file and commit borders stand out.
                FileBarStyle:    str("bold bright-white blue"),
                CommitBarStyle:  str("bold black yellow"),
                CommitHashStyle: str("yellow"),
                BranchStyle:     str("yellow bold"),
                // Moved code as git colors it by default.
                MinusMovedStyle: str("magenta"),
                PlusMovedStyle:  str("cyan"),
                // Trailing whitespace as git marks whitespace errors.
                TrailingWhitespaceStyle: str("normal red"),
                HiddenCharStyle:         str("normal red"),
                OverflowStyle:           str("blue"),
                SearchStyle:             str("black yellow"),
                // Links as browsers draw them.
                DocLinkStyle:         str("bright-blue bold"),
                HeadingStyle:         str("bold"),
                InactiveHeadingStyle: str("dim"),
                KeyStyle:             str("bold"),
        }
}

// FromGit returns the theme that delta's and jdiff's git config sections in
// cfg define with the given features (see Features): an option in [delta]
// wins over the features in their order, jdiff's options are read from
// [jdiff]. As in delta, any key of [delta] can name a color.
func FromGit(cfg Config, features []string) Spec {
        s := Spec{Name: "delta", Kind: KindGit, Colors: map[string]string{}}
        s.options(func(key, section string, field **string) {
                if section == "jdiff" {
                        if v, ok := cfg["jdiff."+key]; ok {
                                *field = &v
                        }
                } else if v, ok := lookup(cfg, features, key); ok {
                        *field = &v
                }
        })
        for k, v := range cfg {
                if name, ok := strings.CutPrefix(k, "delta."); ok && !strings.Contains(name, ".") {
                        s.Colors[name] = v
                }
        }
        return s
}

// FromFile reads the theme file path, a TOML file of options and a [colors]
// table, and names the theme after the file. It also returns the keys of the
// file that are no options.
func FromFile(path string) (Spec, []string, error) {
        s := Spec{Name: strings.TrimSuffix(filepath.Base(path), ".toml"), Kind: KindFile}
        md, err := toml.DecodeFile(path, &s)
        if err != nil {
                return Spec{}, nil, err
        }
        var unknown []string
        for _, k := range md.Undecoded() {
                unknown = append(unknown, k.String())
        }
        // Color words are matched lowercased, as git lowercases its keys.
        colors := make(map[string]string, len(s.Colors))
        for name, c := range s.Colors {
                colors[strings.ToLower(name)] = c
        }
        s.Colors = colors
        return s, unknown, nil
}

package theme

import (
        "fmt"
        "image/color"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
)

// ansiNames are delta's names for the 16 ANSI colors.
var ansiNames = map[string]int{
        "black": 0, "red": 1, "green": 2, "yellow": 3,
        "blue": 4, "magenta": 5, "purple": 5, "cyan": 6, "white": 7,
}

// ParseStyle parses a delta style string: attributes, then an optional
// foreground and background color. Color words that are neither a known
// color nor "normal" are looked up in named, like delta does.
// Words that only matter to delta (omit, raw, box, ...) are ignored.
func ParseStyle(s string, named map[string]string) (lipgloss.Style, error) {
        style := lipgloss.NewStyle()
        colors := 0
        for _, word := range strings.Fields(strings.ToLower(s)) {
                word = strings.Trim(word, `"'`)
                switch word {
                case "bold":
                        style = style.Bold(true)
                case "dim":
                        style = style.Faint(true)
                case "italic":
                        style = style.Italic(true)
                case "ul", "underline":
                        style = style.Underline(true)
                case "blink":
                        style = style.Blink(true)
                case "reverse":
                        style = style.Reverse(true)
                case "strike":
                        style = style.Strikethrough(true)
                case "hidden", "omit", "raw", "line-number", "file", "omit-code-fragment", "box", "ol", "none":
                default:
                        c, err := parseColor(word, named)
                        if err != nil {
                                return lipgloss.NewStyle(), fmt.Errorf("style %q: %w", s, err)
                        }
                        switch colors {
                        case 0:
                                if c != nil {
                                        style = style.Foreground(c)
                                }
                        case 1:
                                if c != nil {
                                        style = style.Background(c)
                                }
                        default:
                                return lipgloss.NewStyle(), fmt.Errorf("style %q: more than two colors", s)
                        }
                        colors++
                }
        }
        return style, nil
}

// parseColor returns the color for word, or nil for no color. Colors beyond
// the 16 ANSI ones are passed on as-is; the 16 color profile of the program
// maps them to the nearest ANSI color when it draws.
func parseColor(word string, named map[string]string) (color.Color, error) {
        switch word {
        case "normal", "auto", "syntax":
                return nil, nil
        }
        if n, ok := ansiNames[word]; ok {
                return lipgloss.Color(strconv.Itoa(n)), nil
        }
        if base, ok := strings.CutPrefix(word, "bright"); ok {
                if n, ok := ansiNames[strings.TrimPrefix(base, "-")]; ok {
                        return lipgloss.Color(strconv.Itoa(n + 8)), nil
                }
        }
        if n, err := strconv.Atoi(word); err == nil && n >= 0 && n <= 255 {
                return lipgloss.Color(word), nil
        }
        if isHexColor(word) {
                return lipgloss.Color(word), nil
        }
        if v, ok := named[word]; ok && !strings.HasPrefix(word, "delta.") {
                // A named color defined elsewhere in the config; no further nesting.
                return parseColor(strings.ToLower(strings.TrimSpace(v)), nil)
        }
        return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown color %q", word)
}

func isHexColor(s string) bool {
        if len(s) != 7 || s[0] != '#' {
                return false
        }
        _, err := strconv.ParseUint(s[1:], 16, 32)
        return err == nil
}

// Package theme builds jdiff's styles. A theme comes from a source, as a
// Spec: jdiff's defaults, delta's git config settings or a theme file;
// Resolve turns it into the styles, with the defaults for what it leaves
// unset.
//
// jdiff renders in 16 color mode: every color is an ANSI color, so the
// terminal palette decides the actual colors. Hex and 256 colors in a theme
// are mapped to the nearest ANSI color.
package theme

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"

        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
)

// Theme holds all styles of the jdiff views.
type Theme struct {
        Minus, MinusNonEmph, MinusEmph, MinusIndent lipgloss.Style
        Plus, PlusNonEmph, PlusEmph, PlusIndent     lipgloss.Style
        Zero                                        lipgloss.Style

        NumMinus, NumPlus, NumZero lipgloss.Style
        NumLeft, NumRight          lipgloss.Style // separators after the numbers

        Title      lipgloss.Style // file names
        Decoration lipgloss.Style // pane separator
        Hint       lipgloss.Style // status bar

        Anchor, Pending, Cursor, Filler lipgloss.Style
        Whitespace                      lipgloss.Style // unchanged whitespace when shown
        TrailingWhitespace              lipgloss.Style // whitespace at the end of lines when shown
        HiddenChar                      lipgloss.Style // ☠ for invisible and direction changing characters
        Overflow                        lipgloss.Style // « and » at the edges of cut lines
        More                            lipgloss.Style // ↑ and ↓ for items of lists out of view
        Search                          lipgloss.Style // text found by /
        MinusMoved, PlusMoved           lipgloss.Style // moved code: removed here, added there
        FileBar, CommitBar              lipgloss.Style // flow mode: start of a file, a commit
        CommitHash                      lipgloss.Style // commit hashes in the log
        Branch                          lipgloss.Style // branch above the commit list
        DocHeading, DocCode, DocLink    lipgloss.Style // --readme: headings, code, links
        Heading                         lipgloss.Style // headings of lists and boxes, the active pane's title
        InactiveHeading                 lipgloss.Style // the inactive pane's title, side by side
        Key                             lipgloss.Style // the keys in the help

        AddedLabel, RemovedLabel, ModifiedLabel, RenamedLabel string

        // Warnings lists settings that could not be used.
        Warnings []string
}

// Default returns the theme without any configuration.
func Default() *Theme { return Resolve(Builtin()) }

// Resolve builds the theme that sp defines. Options sp leaves unset or sets
// to an invalid value take their default; invalid values are reported in
// Warnings. It lists the options one per line, in the order of Theme, on
// purpose: a new option is one more line, the helpers are those of resolver.
func Resolve(sp Spec) *Theme {
        def := Builtin()
        t := &Theme{}
        r := &resolver{sp: sp, t: t}

        t.Minus = r.style("minus-style", sp.MinusStyle, def.MinusStyle)
        t.MinusEmph = r.style("minus-emph-style", sp.MinusEmphStyle, def.MinusEmphStyle)
        t.Plus = r.style("plus-style", sp.PlusStyle, def.PlusStyle)
        t.PlusEmph = r.style("plus-emph-style", sp.PlusEmphStyle, def.PlusEmphStyle)
        t.MinusNonEmph = r.styleOr("minus-non-emph-style", sp.MinusNonEmphStyle, "minus-style", sp.MinusStyle, def.MinusStyle)
        t.PlusNonEmph = r.styleOr("plus-non-emph-style", sp.PlusNonEmphStyle, "plus-style", sp.PlusStyle, def.PlusStyle)
        t.Zero = r.style("zero-style", sp.ZeroStyle, def.ZeroStyle)

        t.NumMinus = r.style("line-numbers-minus-style", sp.LineNumbersMinusStyle, def.LineNumbersMinusStyle)
        t.NumPlus = r.style("line-numbers-plus-style", sp.LineNumbersPlusStyle, def.LineNumbersPlusStyle)
        t.NumZero = r.style("line-numbers-zero-style", sp.LineNumbersZeroStyle, def.LineNumbersZeroStyle)
        t.NumLeft = r.style("line-numbers-left-style", sp.LineNumbersLeftStyle, def.LineNumbersLeftStyle)
        t.NumRight = r.style("line-numbers-right-style", sp.LineNumbersRightStyle, def.LineNumbersRightStyle)

        // Only the color of the decoration is used: jdiff draws no underlines.
        decoration := r.style("file-decoration-style", sp.FileDecorationStyle, def.FileDecorationStyle)
        t.Title = r.style("file-style", sp.FileStyle, def.FileStyle)
        t.Decoration = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(decoration.GetForeground())
        t.Hint = r.style("inline-hint-style", sp.InlineHintStyle, def.InlineHintStyle)

        t.AddedLabel = val(sp.FileAddedLabel, def.FileAddedLabel)
        t.RemovedLabel = val(sp.FileRemovedLabel, def.FileRemovedLabel)
        t.ModifiedLabel = val(sp.FileModifiedLabel, def.FileModifiedLabel)
        t.RenamedLabel = val(sp.FileRenamedLabel, def.FileRenamedLabel)

        t.Anchor = r.style("anchor-style", sp.AnchorStyle, def.AnchorStyle)
        t.Pending = r.style("pending-style", sp.PendingStyle, def.PendingStyle)
        t.Cursor = r.style("cursor-style", sp.CursorStyle, def.CursorStyle)
        t.FileBar = r.style("file-bar-style", sp.FileBarStyle, def.FileBarStyle)
        t.CommitBar = r.style("commit-bar-style", sp.CommitBarStyle, def.CommitBarStyle)
        t.CommitHash = r.style("commit-hash-style", sp.CommitHashStyle, def.CommitHashStyle)
        t.Branch = r.style("branch-style", sp.BranchStyle, def.BranchStyle)
        t.MinusMoved = r.style("minus-moved-style", sp.MinusMovedStyle, def.MinusMovedStyle)
        t.PlusMoved = r.style("plus-moved-style", sp.PlusMovedStyle, def.PlusMovedStyle)
        t.Filler = r.styleOrStyle("filler-style", sp.FillerStyle, t.Decoration)
        t.Whitespace = r.styleOrStyle("whitespace-style", sp.WhitespaceStyle, t.Decoration)
        t.TrailingWhitespace = r.style("trailing-whitespace-style", sp.TrailingWhitespaceStyle, def.TrailingWhitespaceStyle)
        t.HiddenChar = r.style("hidden-char-style", sp.HiddenCharStyle, def.HiddenCharStyle)
        t.Overflow = r.style("overflow-style", sp.OverflowStyle, def.OverflowStyle)
        t.More = r.styleOrStyle("more-style", sp.MoreStyle, t.Whitespace)
        t.Search = r.style("search-style", sp.SearchStyle, def.SearchStyle)
        // Changed whitespace is drawn as · and →, by default like changed words.
        t.MinusIndent = r.styleOrStyle("minus-indent-style", sp.MinusIndentStyle, t.MinusEmph)
        t.PlusIndent = r.styleOrStyle("plus-indent-style", sp.PlusIndentStyle, t.PlusEmph)
        t.DocHeading = r.styleOrStyle("doc-heading-style", sp.DocHeadingStyle, t.Branch)
        t.DocCode = r.styleOrStyle("doc-code-style", sp.DocCodeStyle, t.Title)
        t.DocLink = r.style("doc-link-style", sp.DocLinkStyle, def.DocLinkStyle)
        t.Heading = r.style("heading-style", sp.HeadingStyle, def.HeadingStyle)
        t.InactiveHeading = r.style("inactive-heading-style", sp.InactiveHeadingStyle, def.InactiveHeadingStyle)
        t.Key = r.style("key-style", sp.KeyStyle, def.KeyStyle)
        return t
}

// resolver resolves the options of sp into t, reporting invalid values in
// t.Warnings: the helpers of Resolve, which lists the options one per line.
type resolver struct {
        sp Spec
        t  *Theme
}

// name returns how a warning names option key.
func (r *resolver) name(key string) string {
        switch {
        case r.sp.Kind == KindFile:
                return r.sp.Name + ".toml: " + key
        case isJdiffKey(key):
                return "jdiff." + key
        }
        return "delta." + key
}

// val returns v, or the default d when v is unset.
func val(v, d *string) string {
        if v != nil {
                return *v
        }
        return *d
}

// warn reports err of option key once.
func (r *resolver) warn(key string, err error) {
        // Options that default to another one may report its error again.
        if w := fmt.Sprintf("%s: %v", r.name(key), err); !slices.Contains(r.t.Warnings, w) {
                r.t.Warnings = append(r.t.Warnings, w)
        }
}

// parse returns the style s of option key, fallback when s is invalid.
func (r *resolver) parse(key, s, fallback string) lipgloss.Style {
        st, err := ParseStyle(s, r.sp.Colors)
        if err != nil {
                r.warn(key, err)
                st, _ = ParseStyle(fallback, nil)
        }
        return st
}

// style returns option key with value v and default d.
func (r *resolver) style(key string, v, d *string) lipgloss.Style {
        if v != nil {
                return r.parse(key, *v, *d)
        }
        return r.parse(key, *d, "")
}

// styleOr returns option key with value v, which defaults to option fbKey
// with value fv and default fd.
func (r *resolver) styleOr(key string, v *string, fbKey string, fv, fd *string) lipgloss.Style {
        if v != nil {
                return r.parse(key, *v, val(fv, fd))
        }
        return r.style(fbKey, fv, fd)
}

// styleOrStyle returns option key with value v, which defaults to the style
// d, also when it is invalid.
func (r *resolver) styleOrStyle(key string, v *string, d lipgloss.Style) lipgloss.Style {
        if v == nil {
                return d
        }
        st, err := ParseStyle(*v, r.sp.Colors)
        if err != nil {
                r.warn(key, err)
                return d
        }
        return st
}

package ui

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"
        "strings"
        "time"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/source"
        "jdiff/watch"
)

// App is the top-level model of jdiff: it wraps the page shown, whatever it
// is, and keeps what all pages share: the watcher, auto reload, and git diff
// switched to by d, which asks for the arguments of git diff in a popup,
// whose files then take the page's place, as with jdiff diff. When git
// fails, the popup stays open and says why.
type App struct {
        tea.Model           // the view in its wrappers
        view      tea.Model // the view itself
        wrap      func(tea.Model) tea.Model
        cfg       *config.Config
        // w is the watcher the views share, nil where the system has none;
        // limit is the inotify_max_dirs it has.
        w      *watch.Watcher
        limit  int
        auto   autoReload
        popup  diffPopup
        width  int
        height int
}

// diffPopup is the popup of git diff of App: run lists the diff of the
// arguments asked for in input, which is set while it asks; err is why git
// failed, until the next key. running is set while git diff runs for the
// request number gen of show; cancel ends it.
type diffPopup struct {
        run     DiffFunc
        input   *lineInput
        err     string
        running bool
        gen     int
        cancel  context.CancelFunc
}

// NewApp returns the app showing view in the search bar, the help, the
// settings and the marks popups, as it does the views that replace it;
// gitDiff runs git diff.
func NewApp(cfg *config.Config, view tea.Model, gitDiff DiffFunc) *App {
        return newApp(cfg, view, func(m tea.Model) tea.Model {
                return WithSearch(cfg, WithHelp(cfg, WithSettings(cfg, WithMarks(cfg, m))))
        }, gitDiff)
}

// newApp returns the app showing view, which wrap puts in its wrappers, as
// it does the views that replace it. The tests choose the wrappers.
func newApp(cfg *config.Config, view tea.Model, wrap func(tea.Model) tea.Model, gitDiff DiffFunc) *App {
        return &App{Model: wrap(view), view: view, wrap: wrap, cfg: cfg, popup: diffPopup{run: gitDiff}}
}

// WithAutoReload sets how auto reload gets the state of the repository, the
// files to watch for changes of it and the directories of its working tree,
// and returns s.
func (s *App) WithAutoReload(status func(ctx context.Context) (string, error), paths func() ([]string, error),
        dirs func(ignore func(rel string) bool) (map[string]time.Time, error)) *App {
        s.auto.repoStatus, s.auto.repoPaths, s.auto.workDirs = status, paths, dirs
        return s
}

// Init starts the watcher the views share, unless watching is off, and
// gives it to the view, before it starts.
func (s *App) Init() tea.Cmd {
        var wait tea.Cmd
        if s.cfg.Settings.WatchInterval > 0 {
                if w, err := watch.New(s.cfg.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs); err == nil {
                        s.w, s.limit, wait = w, s.cfg.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs, waitChanges(w)
                        s.share()
                }
        }
        return batch(s.Model.Init(), wait)
}

// share gives the watcher to the view, if it watches files.
func (s *App) share() {
        if v, ok := s.view.(interface{ shareWatcher(*watch.Watcher) }); ok && s.w != nil {
                v.shareWatcher(s.w)
        }
}

// current returns the arguments of the git diff shown, and whether the view
// shows one.
func (s *App) current() ([]string, bool) {
        if d, ok := s.view.(*Diff); ok && d.gitDiff != nil {
                return d.diffArgs, true
        }
        return nil, false
}

func (s *App) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                s.width, s.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
        case askDiffMsg:
                s.popup.input, s.popup.err = &lineInput{}, ""
                s.endDiff()
                return s, nil
        case diffListedMsg:
                return s, s.showDiff(msg)
        case askAutoReloadMsg:
                return s, s.toggleAutoReload()
        case autoTickMsg:
                return s, s.onAutoTick(msg)
        case autoStatusMsg:
                return s, s.onAutoStatus(msg)
        case changedMsg:
                if s.w != nil && msg.w == s.w {
                        check := s.onRepoChanged(msg.paths)
                        var cmd tea.Cmd
                        s.Model, cmd = s.Model.Update(msg)
                        return s, batch(cmd, check, waitChanges(s.w))
                }
        case watchEndedMsg:
                if s.w != nil && msg.w == s.w {
                        s.w = nil // the regular checks still see all changes; the view checks its files
                }
        case repoWatchMsg:
                s.onRepoWatch(msg)
                return s, nil
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                s.auto.lastActive = time.Now()
                if s.popup.input != nil {
                        s.popup.err = ""
                        return s, s.key(msg)
                }
        case tea.PasteMsg:
                if s.popup.input != nil {
                        s.popup.err = ""
                        s.popup.input.paste(msg.Content)
                        return s, nil
                }
        }
        var cmd tea.Cmd
        s.Model, cmd = s.Model.Update(msg)
        // Set in the settings popup; Diff and FileDiff applied it already
        // (diskWatch.limit), not the views without files on disk. See "Kept on
        // purpose" in doc/development.md.
        if s.w != nil && s.limit != s.cfg.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs {
                s.limit = s.cfg.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs
                s.w.SetLimit(s.limit)
        }
        return s, cmd
}

// key edits the arguments asked for; all text typed goes into the line. Up
// takes the arguments of the git diff shown to edit. Enter shows the diff of
// the line; with nothing typed it closes the popup, as esc and backspace on
// it do.
func (s *App) key(k tea.KeyPressMsg) tea.Cmd {
        in := s.popup.input
        switch {
        case k.String() == "ctrl+c":
                return tea.Quit
        case in.cancels(k):
                s.popup.input = nil
                s.endDiff()
                return nil
        }
        switch k.String() {
        case "enter":
                args, err := splitArgs(in.text)
                if err != nil {
                        s.popup.err = err.Error()
                        return nil
                }
                if len(args) == 0 {
                        s.popup.input = nil
                        return nil
                }
                return s.show(args)
        case "up":
                if args, ok := s.current(); ok {
                        in.text = joinArgs(args)
                }
        default:
                in.edit(k)
        }
        return nil
}

// splitArgs splits text into arguments at spaces, as a shell does for
// quotes: 'single' and "double" quotes keep spaces, a backslash outside
// single quotes the character after it.
func splitArgs(text string) ([]string, error) {
        var args []string
        var cur strings.Builder
        inArg, quote, escaped := false, rune(0), false
        for _, r := range text {
                switch {
                case escaped:
                        cur.WriteRune(r)
                        escaped = false
                case r == '\\' && quote != '\'':
                        escaped, inArg = true, true
                case quote != 0 && r == quote:
                        quote = 0
                case quote != 0:
                        cur.WriteRune(r)
                case r == '\'' || r == '"':
                        quote, inArg = r, true
                case r == ' ':
                        if inArg {
                                args, inArg = append(args, cur.String()), false
                                cur.Reset()
                        }
                default:
                        cur.WriteRune(r)
                        inArg = true
                }
        }
        if quote != 0 {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("missing closing %c", quote)
        }
        if inArg {
                args = append(args, cur.String())
        }
        return args, nil
}

// joinArgs returns args as splitArgs reads them back: quoted where they hold
// spaces, quotes or backslashes, or are empty.
func joinArgs(args []string) string {
        quoted := make([]string, len(args))
        for i, a := range args {
                quoted[i] = a
                if a == "" || strings.ContainsAny(a, " '\"\\") {
                        quoted[i] = "'" + strings.ReplaceAll(a, "'", `'"'"'`) + "'"
                }
        }
        return strings.Join(quoted, " ")
}

// endDiff ends the git diff of the popup, if one runs: its files are no
// longer wanted.
func (s *App) endDiff() {
        s.popup.running = false
        if s.popup.cancel != nil {
                s.popup.cancel()
                s.popup.cancel = nil
        }
}

// stop ends all git commands of s and its view: the program ends.
func (s *App) stop() {
        s.endDiff()
        s.stopAutoReload()
        if v, ok := s.view.(interface{ stop() }); ok {
                v.stop()
        }
        if s.w != nil {
                s.w.Close()
        }
}

// diffListedMsg brings the files of git diff with args, run for the request
// number gen of show, or why git failed.
type diffListedMsg struct {
        gen   int
        args  []string
        files []source.File
        err   error
}

// show returns the command running git diff with args in the background,
// for showDiff. The popup stays open meanwhile and says so; esc closes it
// and drops the files, as another request does.
func (s *App) show(args []string) tea.Cmd {
        s.endDiff()
        s.popup.gen++
        s.popup.running = true
        ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
        s.popup.cancel = cancel
        gen, untracked, gitDiff := s.popup.gen, s.cfg.Settings.Untracked, s.popup.run
        return func() tea.Msg {
                files, err := gitDiff(ctx, args, untracked)
                return diffListedMsg{gen, args, files, err}
        }
}

// showDiff replaces the view by the files msg brings, from scratch, closes
// the popup and returns the command starting the new view; when the popup was
// closed or asked again meanwhile, it drops them. When git failed, the view
// and the popup stay, which shows the first line of git's message.
func (s *App) showDiff(msg diffListedMsg) tea.Cmd {
        if msg.gen != s.popup.gen || !s.popup.running { // closed or opened again meanwhile
                return nil
        }
        s.endDiff()
        args, files := msg.args, msg.files
        if msg.err != nil {
                s.popup.err = firstLine(msg.err)
                return nil
        }
        s.popup.input = nil
        if v, ok := s.view.(interface{ stop() }); ok {
                v.stop()
        }
        d := NewDiff(s.cfg, files).WithGitDiff(args, s.popup.run)
        if len(files) > 0 {
                d.setMsg(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %d files", diffCommand(args), len(files)))
        }
        s.view, s.Model = d, s.wrap(d)
        s.share()
        s.Model.Update(tea.WindowSizeMsg{Width: s.width, Height: s.height})
        cmd := s.Model.Init()
        if s.cfg.AutoReload && !s.auto.gitMode { // now git diff: checked by git status
                s.stopAutoReload()
                cmd = batch(cmd, s.toggleAutoReload())
        }
        return cmd
}

// frame draws the popup over the view while it asks: the line typed,
// centered, and why git failed. The popup grows with the line, up to the
// width of the screen; beyond, the line's start is cut so that its end
// stays in view.
func (s *App) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(s.frame()) }

func (s *App) frame() string {
        base := s.Model.View().Content
        if s.popup.input == nil || s.width == 0 {
                return base
        }
        t := s.cfg.Theme
        keys := "enter show · esc cancel"
        if _, ok := s.current(); ok {
                keys = "enter show · ↑ current · esc cancel"
        }
        msg := ""
        if s.popup.running {
                msg = "running git diff…"
        }
        pop, w := inputBox(t, s.width, popupTitle(t, "git diff", msg, keys), printable(s.popup.input.text), s.popup.err, true)
        return overlay(base, pop, w, s.width)
}

package ui

import (
        "context"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "path/filepath"
        "slices"
        "strings"
        "time"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
)

// autoReload is the state of auto reload of App: repoStatus returns the
// state of the repository, which is checked, while gitMode is set, every
// auto_reload_interval and at once when App's watcher reports a change of the
// files of repoPaths, pinned, or in the directories of workDirs. statusRuns
// counts the checks under way, statusAgain asks for one more after them; tick
// numbers the regular checks, of which only the last counts; gen tells this
// run of auto reload from earlier ones; ctx ends its git commands, cancel
// ends ctx.
type autoReload struct {
        repoStatus  func(ctx context.Context) (string, error)
        repoPaths   func() ([]string, error)
        workDirs    func(ignore func(rel string) bool) (map[string]time.Time, error)
        gitMode     bool
        tick        int
        pins        []string
        statusAgain bool
        lastStatus  string
        lastActive  time.Time
        statusRuns  int
        gen         int
        ctx         context.Context
        cancel      context.CancelFunc
}

// autoReloadMsg tells the view that auto reload went on or off: it watches
// its files on disk only while it is on.
type autoReloadMsg struct{ on bool }

// autoTickMsg asks App for the next check of auto reload number gen,
// the tick number tick of it; a later or ended one drops it, and so does a
// change reported, which starts the next tick anew.
type autoTickMsg struct{ gen, tick int }

// autoStatusMsg is the result of git status for auto reload number gen.
type autoStatusMsg struct {
        gen    int
        status string
        err    error
}

// toggleAutoReload starts or stops auto reload and returns the commands of
// its checks. On, the view watches its files on disk. Of git diff and git
// log, the repository is watched and checked by git status too, and what is
// shown loaded again at once, since the repository may have changed since
// the last load; of two files or directories, only the files shown are
// watched (gitMode off), and the ticks only end it after the timeout. When it
// cannot start, the view says why.
func (s *App) toggleAutoReload() tea.Cmd {
        if s.cfg.AutoReload {
                s.stopAutoReload()
                return nil
        }
        s.auto.gitMode = s.auto.repoStatus != nil && s.gitView()
        if s.auto.gitMode {
                if s.cfg.Settings.AutoReloadInterval <= 0 {
                        return showAutoReloadError(errors.New("auto_reload_interval is 0"))
                }
                ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
                status, err := s.auto.repoStatus(ctx)
                if err != nil {
                        cancel()
                        return showAutoReloadError(err)
                }
                s.auto.ctx, s.auto.cancel, s.auto.lastStatus = ctx, cancel, status
        }
        s.cfg.AutoReload, s.auto.lastActive = true, time.Now()
        s.auto.gen++
        var on, cmd tea.Cmd
        s.Model, on = s.Model.Update(autoReloadMsg{on: true})
        if s.auto.gitMode {
                s.Model, cmd = s.Model.Update(refreshMsg{quiet: true})
        }
        return batch(cmd, on, s.autoTick(), s.watchRepo())
}

// gitView reports whether the view shows git diff or git log, which auto
// reload checks by git status.
func (s *App) gitView() bool {
        _, diff := s.current()
        _, log := s.view.(*Log)
        return diff || log
}

// stopAutoReload ends auto reload: its git commands are killed, and none
// run for it from now on; nothing is watched.
func (s *App) stopAutoReload() {
        s.cfg.AutoReload = false
        s.auto.gen++
        if s.auto.cancel != nil {
                s.auto.cancel()
                s.auto.cancel = nil
        }
        if s.w != nil {
                s.w.Pin(nil)
                s.w.SetDirs(nil)
        }
        s.auto.pins, s.auto.statusAgain, s.auto.gitMode = nil, false, false
        s.Model, _ = s.Model.Update(autoReloadMsg{on: false})
}

// autoTick returns the command for the next regular check, nil without an
// interval.
func (s *App) autoTick() tea.Cmd {
        if s.cfg.Settings.AutoReloadInterval <= 0 {
                return nil
        }
        s.auto.tick++
        gen, tick := s.auto.gen, s.auto.tick
        every := time.Duration(s.cfg.Settings.AutoReloadInterval) * time.Second
        return tea.Tick(every, func(time.Time) tea.Msg { return autoTickMsg{gen, tick} })
}

// checkStatus returns the command running git status.
func (s *App) checkStatus() tea.Cmd {
        s.auto.statusRuns++
        gen, status, ctx := s.auto.gen, s.auto.repoStatus, s.auto.ctx
        return func() tea.Msg {
                st, err := status(ctx)
                return autoStatusMsg{gen, st, err}
        }
}

// onAutoTick ends auto reload after the timeout without activity, else checks
// the status, unless git is still at it, as in a large repository, or there
// is no git status to check.
func (s *App) onAutoTick(msg autoTickMsg) tea.Cmd {
        if msg.gen != s.auto.gen || msg.tick != s.auto.tick || !s.cfg.AutoReload {
                return nil
        }
        // In minutes as float: a large timeout overflows a time.Duration.
        if m := s.cfg.Settings.AutoReloadTimeout; m > 0 && time.Since(s.auto.lastActive).Minutes() >= float64(m) {
                s.stopAutoReload()
                return func() tea.Msg { return showMsg(fmt.Sprintf("auto reload off: %d min without keys or changes", m)) }
        }
        if s.auto.statusRuns > 0 || !s.auto.gitMode {
                return s.autoTick()
        }
        return tea.Batch(s.autoTick(), s.checkStatus())
}

// onAutoStatus loads what is shown again when the status differs from the last
// one, which counts as activity, and watches the repository anew, whose files
// to watch it may have changed. The directories of the files it names anew
// count as used. git status elsewhere, or git diff refreshing the index,
// makes the watcher report a change but leaves the status as it is. When git
// fails, auto reload ends. A change reported while git was at it is checked
// once more.
func (s *App) onAutoStatus(msg autoStatusMsg) tea.Cmd {
        s.auto.statusRuns--
        if msg.gen != s.auto.gen {
                return nil
        }
        if msg.err != nil {
                s.stopAutoReload()
                return showAutoReloadError(msg.err)
        }
        var again tea.Cmd
        if s.auto.statusAgain && s.auto.statusRuns == 0 {
                s.auto.statusAgain, again = false, s.checkStatus()
        }
        if msg.status == s.auto.lastStatus {
                return again
        }
        if s.w != nil {
                var paths []string
                for _, p := range gitsrc.StatusPaths(msg.status, s.auto.lastStatus) {
                        if abs, err := filepath.Abs(p); err == nil { // relative to where git ran
                                paths = append(paths, abs)
                        }
                }
                s.w.Touch(paths)
        }
        s.auto.lastStatus, s.auto.lastActive = msg.status, time.Now()
        var cmd tea.Cmd
        s.Model, cmd = s.Model.Update(refreshMsg{quiet: true})
        return batch(cmd, s.watchRepo(), again)
}

// onRepoChanged checks the status at once when the watcher reported a change
// of the repository or of the working tree, unless only of files that
// watch_ignore matches; while git is at it, once more after. The next regular
// check comes a full interval later. Without git status, a change of the
// files shown counts as activity.
func (s *App) onRepoChanged(paths []string) tea.Cmd {
        if !s.cfg.AutoReload {
                return nil
        }
        if !s.auto.gitMode {
                s.auto.lastActive = time.Now()
                return nil
        }
        if !slices.ContainsFunc(paths, s.repoChange) {
                return nil
        }
        if s.auto.statusRuns > 0 {
                s.auto.statusAgain = true
                return s.autoTick()
        }
        return batch(s.checkStatus(), s.autoTick())
}

// repoChange reports whether a change of path counts for auto reload: of the
// pinned files of the repository, or in the working tree, but of those that
// watch_ignore matches.
func (s *App) repoChange(path string) bool {
        if slices.Contains(s.auto.pins, path) {
                return true
        }
        rel := path
        if top := s.cfg.Repo(); top != "" {
                r, err := filepath.Rel(top, path)
                if err != nil || r == ".." || strings.HasPrefix(r, ".."+string(filepath.Separator)) {
                        return false
                }
                rel = r
        }
        return !s.cfg.WatchIgnored(filepath.ToSlash(rel))
}

// repoWatchMsg brings, for auto reload number gen, the files of the
// repository to watch and the directories of its working tree.
type repoWatchMsg struct {
        gen  int
        pins []string
        dirs map[string]time.Time
}

// watchRepo returns the command finding in the background what auto reload
// watches of git: the files of the repository, which a checkout changes, and
// the directories of the working tree, unless inotify_max_dirs is 0; nil
// without a watcher or git. When git fails,
// the watcher keeps what it watched: the regular checks still see all
// changes, only later.
func (s *App) watchRepo() tea.Cmd {
        if s.w == nil || s.auto.repoPaths == nil || !s.auto.gitMode {
                return nil
        }
        gen, paths, workDirs, ignored := s.auto.gen, s.auto.repoPaths, s.auto.workDirs, s.cfg.WatchIgnored
        // Without directories to watch, the working tree is not looked at.
        scan := workDirs != nil && s.cfg.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs > 0
        return func() tea.Msg {
                pins, err := paths()
                if err != nil {
                        return nil
                }
                var dirs map[string]time.Time
                if scan {
                        if dirs, err = workDirs(ignored); err != nil {
                                return nil
                        }
                }
                return repoWatchMsg{gen, pins, dirs}
        }
}

// onRepoWatch watches what msg brings, the files of the repository always,
// unless auto reload ended meanwhile.
func (s *App) onRepoWatch(msg repoWatchMsg) {
        if msg.gen != s.auto.gen || !s.cfg.AutoReload || s.w == nil {
                return
        }
        s.auto.pins = msg.pins
        s.w.Pin(msg.pins)
        s.w.SetDirs(msg.dirs)
}

// showAutoReloadError returns the command showing why auto reload is off.
func showAutoReloadError(err error) tea.Cmd {
        return func() tea.Msg { return showMsg("auto reload: " + firstLine(err)) }
}

package ui

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
        "jdiff/internal/source"
        "jdiff/internal/theme"
        "jdiff/watch"
)

// fileEntry is one file in the list. Its alignment is created with the
// list, since moved code is searched in all files together, and its diff
// view on first open; both are kept, so anchors survive going back to the
// list.
type fileEntry struct {
        source.File
        added, removed int
        align          *fileAlign
        view           *FileDiff
}

// label returns the status label, using delta's file labels.
func (e *fileEntry) label(th *theme.Theme) string {
        switch {
        case e.OldPath != "":
                return th.RenamedLabel
        case !e.InOld:
                return th.AddedLabel
        case !e.InNew:
                return th.RemovedLabel
        }
        return th.ModifiedLabel
}

// name returns the path shown for the file, "old → new" for renames,
// printable: paths may hold control characters.
func (e *fileEntry) name() string {
        if e.OldPath != "" {
                return printable(e.OldPath) + " → " + printable(e.Path)
        }
        return printable(e.Path)
}

// paneNames returns the names shown above the panes of the file's diff.
func (e *fileEntry) paneNames() [2]string {
        return [2]string{"a/" + e.PathInOld(), "b/" + e.Path}
}

// refresh reloads the sides of e that changed on disk, see refreshFile. Its
// diff view and after, unless nil, follow each reload of its diff.
func (e *fileEntry) refresh(after func(*fileAlign)) string {
        if e.Binary {
                return ""
        }
        msg := refreshFile(&e.File, e.align, func() { e.followReload(after) })
        if msg != "" {
                e.updateStats()
        }
        return msg
}

// update takes f, the file listed again, into e, reloading the sides of
// its diff whose content changed. Its diff view and after, unless nil,
// follow each reload, as in refresh. It returns the anchors dropped and
// whether the content changed.
func (e *fileEntry) update(f source.File, after func(*fileAlign)) (dropped int, changed bool) {
        was := [2][]string{e.Old, e.New}
        e.File = f
        if e.Binary {
                return 0, false
        }
        e.align.names = e.paneNames()
        for s, lines := range [2][]string{f.Old, f.New} {
                if slices.Equal(was[s], lines) {
                        continue
                }
                changed = true
                dropped += e.align.reload(side(s), lines)
                e.followReload(after)
        }
        return dropped, changed
}

// followReload lets its diff view and after, unless nil, follow a reload of
// its diff.
func (e *fileEntry) followReload(after func(*fileAlign)) {
        if e.view != nil {
                e.view.syncLayout()
        }
        if after != nil {
                after(e.align)
        }
}

func (e *fileEntry) updateStats() {
        if e.Binary {
                return
        }
        if e.align != nil {
                e.added, e.removed = diff.Stats(e.align.rows)
                return
        }
        e.added, e.removed = diff.Stats(diff.Align(e.Old, e.New, nil))
}

// fileListRows is the most rows the file list takes while the preview shows,
// so that on a tall screen the preview gets the rest.
const fileListRows = 15

// Diff is the Bubble Tea model of the file list (keys of file_list), of jdiff
// diff, two directories and a commit of the log. It shows the diff view
// of the selected file and returns to the list on backspace. A single file
// has its list too, as many do.
type Diff struct {
        cfg       *config.Config
        commit    *gitsrc.Commit // whose files are shown, nil outside the log
        files     []*fileEntry
        relist    ListFunc    // lists the files again, nil when they cannot be
        relisting relistState // of the files, see relistFiles
        // ctx ends the git commands of the view, cancel ends ctx, see stop.
        ctx    context.Context
        cancel context.CancelFunc
        // gitDiff runs git diff with arguments, nil outside jdiff diff; diffArgs
        // are those of the files shown.
        gitDiff  DiffFunc
        diffArgs []string
        // disk watches the files on disk while auto reload is on, from
        // watchFiles on; those near the cursor that it does not watch are checked
        // every watch_interval. touched is the file the cursor was on last.
        disk     diskWatch
        watching bool
        touched  *fileEntry
        list
        // previewTop is the first screen line of the preview scrolled by pgup
        // and pgdn, while previewFor is the file under the cursor; moving the
        // cursor drops it.
        previewFor *fileEntry
        previewTop int
        open       *fileEntry // file whose diff view is shown, nil for the list
        flow       *Flow      // flow view outside the log, created on first use
        msg        string
        prefix     string // the action, "jump", while waiting for the letter of a mark
}

// NewDiff returns the file list for files.
func NewDiff(cfg *config.Config, files []source.File) *Diff { return newDiff(cfg, files, nil) }

// ListFunc lists the files of a comparison again. It runs in the background,
// and ends when ctx is done.
type ListFunc func(ctx context.Context) ([]source.File, error)

// DiffFunc returns the files git diff compares with args, with the
// untracked files as added when untracked is set. It runs in the background:
// it must not read the settings, which the keys change meanwhile; it ends
// when ctx is done.
type DiffFunc func(ctx context.Context, args []string, untracked bool) ([]source.File, error)

// WithGitDiff makes d the files of git diff with args, which r runs again,
// and returns d.
func (d *Diff) WithGitDiff(args []string, gitDiff DiffFunc) *Diff {
        d.gitDiff, d.diffArgs = gitDiff, args
        return d
}

// WithRelist sets how r lists the files again and returns d.
func (d *Diff) WithRelist(list ListFunc) *Diff {
        d.relist = list
        return d
}

// newDiff returns the file list for files, as part of the log for the
// files of commit when it is set.
func newDiff(cfg *config.Config, files []source.File, commit *gitsrc.Commit) *Diff {
        d := &Diff{cfg: cfg, commit: commit}
        d.ctx, d.cancel = context.WithCancel(context.Background())
        // The files are searched for moved code together.
        group := &moveGroup{}
        for _, f := range files {
                d.addEntry(group, &fileEntry{File: f})
        }
        d.list = list{n: len(d.files), preview: &cfg.Settings.Preview, maxRows: fileListRows}
        if len(files) == 0 {
                d.msg = "no changed files"
        }
        if w := cfg.Warning(); w != "" {
                d.msg = w
        }
        return d
}

// addEntry adds e to the files and its diff, unless binary, to group, the
// files searched for moved code together.
func (d *Diff) addEntry(group *moveGroup, e *fileEntry) {
        if !e.Binary {
                group.add(d.diffOf(e), [2]string{e.PathInOld(), e.Path})
        }
        e.updateStats()
        d.files = append(d.files, e)
}

// stop stops watching the files and ends the git commands of the view: it
// is replaced, or the program ends.
func (d *Diff) stop() {
        d.cancel()
        d.disk.stop()
}

// shareWatcher makes d watch its files by w, which App shares.
func (d *Diff) shareWatcher(w *watch.Watcher) { d.disk.share(w) }

// diffCommand returns the git diff command line of args, quoted as the
// popup of git diff reads them.
func diffCommand(args []string) string {
        return strings.TrimSpace("git diff " + joinArgs(args))
}

// inLog reports whether a is part of the log: backspace and N/P go to the
// log.
func (d *Diff) inLog() bool { return d.commit != nil }

// openPath opens the file with the given path and reports whether it could.
func (d *Diff) openPath(path string) bool {
        i := d.fileByPath(path)
        if i >= 0 {
                d.openIndex(i)
        }
        return i >= 0
}

// fileByPath returns the index of the file with the given path that can be
// shown, or -1.
func (d *Diff) fileByPath(path string) int {
        return slices.IndexFunc(d.files, func(e *fileEntry) bool { return e.Path == path && !e.Binary })
}

// fileByGroup returns the index of the file at index i of the files searched
// for moved code, or -1.
func (d *Diff) fileByGroup(i int) int {
        return slices.IndexFunc(d.files, func(e *fileEntry) bool { return e.align != nil && e.align.groupIndex == i })
}
