// openIndex opens file i, after updating the counts of the file it leaves.
func (d *Diff) openIndex(i int) {
        if d.open != nil {
                d.open.updateStats()
        }
        d.cursor = i
        d.scrollToCursor()
        d.openFile()
}

// setMsg shows msg in the status bar of whatever is displayed.
func (d *Diff) setMsg(msg string) {
        switch {
        case d.flowShown():
                d.flow.msg = msg
        case d.open != nil:
                d.open.view.msg = msg
        default:
                d.msg = msg
        }
}

// Init starts watching the files, outside the log.
func (d *Diff) Init() tea.Cmd {
        if d.inLog() {
                return nil
        }
        return d.watchFiles()
}

// watchFiles watches the files on disk, in place of those before, while auto
// reload is on, and returns the command waiting for their changes and the
// check of those not watched; off, it watches none.
func (d *Diff) watchFiles() tea.Cmd {
        if !d.cfg.AutoReload {
                d.disk.stop()
                d.watching = false
                return nil
        }
        var paths []string
        for _, e := range d.files {
                paths = append(paths, diskPaths(e.File)...)
        }
        cmd, msg := d.disk.set(d.cfg, paths)
        if msg != "" {
                d.setMsg(msg)
        }
        d.watching = true
        // The cursor's file counts as used from the start, not only from the
        // next message on: under inotify_max_dirs its directories go first.
        return batch(cmd, d.followCursor(), d.poll())
}

// poll returns the next check of the files near the cursor, the current one
// and its neighbors, that are not watched; nil while one is pending, or when
// none is to be checked.
func (d *Diff) poll() tea.Cmd {
        var paths []string
        for _, i := range d.nearCursor() {
                paths = append(paths, diskPaths(d.files[i].File)...)
        }
        return d.disk.poll(d.cfg, d, paths)
}

// nearCursor returns the indices of the current file and its neighbors.
func (d *Diff) nearCursor() []int {
        var out []int
        if cur := d.current(); cur >= 0 {
                for i := max(cur-1, 0); i <= min(cur+1, len(d.files)-1); i++ {
                        out = append(out, i)
                }
        }
        return out
}

// unwatched reports whether a side of file i on disk is not watched.
func (d *Diff) unwatched(i int) bool {
        return d.disk.unwatched(diskPaths(d.files[i].File))
}

// current returns the index of the file the cursor is on: in the flow view,
// the diff view or the list; -1 for none.
func (d *Diff) current() int {
        if d.flowShown() {
                _, file, _ := d.flow.position()
                return file
        }
        if d.open != nil {
                return slices.Index(d.files, d.open)
        }
        if d.cursor < len(d.files) {
                return d.cursor
        }
        return -1
}

// followCursor counts the directories of the file the cursor is on as used
// when it is on another one than before, and reloads it when it changed on
// disk, as it may while its directory was not watched. It returns the check
// of the files near the cursor not watched.
func (d *Diff) followCursor() tea.Cmd {
        i := d.current()
        if !d.watching || !d.cfg.AutoReload || i < 0 || d.files[i] == d.touched {
                return nil
        }
        d.touched = d.files[i]
        d.disk.touch(diskPaths(d.touched.File))
        f := d.touched.File
        if f.OldFile != "" && stale(f.OldFile, f.OldStamp) || f.NewFile != "" && stale(f.NewFile, f.NewStamp) {
                d.refresh(func(j int) bool { return j == i })
        }
        return d.poll()
}

// refresh reloads the files i that changed on disk for which which holds.
func (d *Diff) refresh(which func(i int) bool) {
        var msgs []string
        reload := func(after func(*fileAlign)) {
                for i := range d.files {
                        if !which(i) {
                                continue
                        }
                        if msg := d.files[i].refresh(after); msg != "" {
                                msgs = append(msgs, msg)
                        }
                }
        }
        if d.flowShown() {
                d.flow.refresh(reload)
        } else {
                reload(nil)
        }
        if len(msgs) > 0 {
                d.setMsg(strings.Join(msgs, "; "))
        }
}

// filesListedMsg brings the files d listed again, or why it could not.
type filesListedMsg struct {
        diff  *Diff
        files []source.File
        err   error
        quiet bool
}

// relistFiles returns the command listing the files again (r) in the
// background: git diff, or the two directories. The files stay usable
// meanwhile. While a listing runs, another one is not started, but run once
// it is done, since what to list may have changed, as the untracked files.
// With quiet, as auto reload asks, nothing is said unless something changed.
func (d *Diff) relistFiles(quiet bool) tea.Cmd {
        list := d.relist
        if d.gitDiff != nil {
                args, untracked, gitDiff := d.diffArgs, d.cfg.Settings.Untracked, d.gitDiff
                list = func(ctx context.Context) ([]source.File, error) { return gitDiff(ctx, args, untracked) }
        }
        if list == nil {
                d.setMsg(nothingToReload)
                return nil
        }
        if !d.relisting.start(quiet) {
                return nil
        }
        if !quiet {
                d.setMsg(reloading)
        }
        ctx := d.ctx
        return func() tea.Msg {
                files, err := list(ctx)
                return filesListedMsg{d, files, err, quiet}
        }
}

// filesListed takes the files listed again. New files are added and gone
// ones dropped; a file still there keeps its entry, and so its diff with
// anchors and folds, reloaded where its content changed. A file that became
// binary or stopped being binary counts as gone and new. The cursor stays on
// its file; when the file shown is gone, the list is shown. The files are
// watched anew; it returns the command waiting for their changes, and the
// one listing them once more when that was asked for meanwhile.
func (d *Diff) filesListed(res filesListedMsg) tea.Cmd {
        var next tea.Cmd
        if again, quiet := d.relisting.done(); again {
                next = d.relistFiles(quiet)
        }
        files, quiet := res.files, res.quiet
        if res.err != nil {
                d.setMsg(firstLine(res.err))
                return next
        }
        kept := map[string]*fileEntry{}
        for _, e := range d.files {
                kept[e.Path] = e
        }
        var cur *fileEntry
        if d.cursor < len(d.files) {
                cur = d.files[d.cursor]
        }
        added, changed, dropped := 0, 0, 0
        update := func(after func(*fileAlign)) {
                old := d.files
                d.files = nil
                // The files are searched for moved code together.
                group := &moveGroup{}
                for _, f := range files {
                        e := kept[f.Path]
                        if e == nil || e.Binary != f.Binary {
                                e = &fileEntry{File: f}
                                added++
                        } else {
                                delete(kept, f.Path)
                                n, ok := e.update(f, after)
                                if ok {
                                        changed++
                                }
                                dropped += n
                        }
                        d.addEntry(group, e)
                }
                if d.flow != nil {
                        d.flow.followFiles(0, old, d.files)
                }
        }
        shown := d.flowShown()
        if shown {
                d.flow.refresh(update)
        } else {
                update(nil)
        }
        d.list.n = len(d.files)
        if i := slices.Index(d.files, cur); i >= 0 {
                d.cursor = i
        } else {
                d.cursor = max(min(d.cursor, len(d.files)-1), 0)
        }
        msg := refreshed(added, len(kept), changed, dropped)
        switch {
        case len(d.files) == 0:
                d.open = nil
        case shown:
                if _, file, _ := d.flow.position(); file >= 0 {
                        d.cursor = file
                }
                d.open = d.files[d.cursor]
        case d.open != nil && !slices.Contains(d.files, d.open):
                msg = d.open.Path + " is gone; " + msg
                d.open = nil
        }
        d.scrollToCursor()
        if !quiet || msg != nothingChanged {
                d.setMsg(msg)
        }
        if d.inLog() {
                return next
        }
        return batch(d.watchFiles(), next)
}

// batch returns the commands that are not nil, as one: tea.Batch of them
// when there are several, so that a single command stays plain, as the
// tests run them.
func batch(cmds ...tea.Cmd) tea.Cmd {
        cmds = slices.DeleteFunc(cmds, func(c tea.Cmd) bool { return c == nil })
        switch len(cmds) {
        case 0:
                return nil
        case 1:
                return cmds[0]
        }
        return tea.Batch(cmds...)
}

// flowShown reports whether the flow view is shown instead of a diff view.
// Inside the log, the log shows its own flow view.
func (d *Diff) flowShown() bool { return d.open != nil && d.cfg.Settings.Flow && !d.inLog() }

// Update handles msg, then follows the cursor, see followCursor.
func (d *Diff) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        m, cmd := d.update(msg)
        return m, batch(cmd, d.followCursor())
}

func (d *Diff) update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                d.width, d.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                d.scrollToCursor()
        case settingChangedMsg:
                d.scrollToCursor() // the preview takes room from the list
                if msg.problem != "" && d.open == nil {
                        d.msg = msg.problem
                }
                if msg.action == "inotify_max_dirs" {
                        d.disk.limit(d.cfg)
                        return d, d.poll()
                }
        case autoReloadMsg:
                if d.inLog() {
                        return d, nil
                }
                if msg.on { // changed while off
                        d.touched = nil
                        d.refresh(func(int) bool { return true })
                }
                return d, d.watchFiles()
        case watchTickMsg:
                if !d.disk.ticked(d.cfg, d, msg) {
                        return d, nil
                }
                near := d.nearCursor()
                d.refresh(func(i int) bool { return slices.Contains(near, i) && d.unwatched(i) })
                return d, d.poll()
        case changedMsg:
                if msg.w == nil || msg.w != d.disk.w {
                        return d, nil
                }
                changed := func(i int) bool {
                        return slices.ContainsFunc(diskPaths(d.files[i].File), func(p string) bool { return slices.Contains(msg.paths, p) })
                }
                d.refresh(changed)
                return d, batch(d.disk.next(), d.poll())
        case watchEndedMsg:
                if !d.disk.ended(msg.w) {
                        return d, nil
                }
                return d, d.poll()
        case jumpMsg:
                d.jump(msg)
                return d, nil
        case markJumpMsg:
                d.gotoMark(msg.letter, msg.screenRow)
                return d, nil
        case switchFileMsg:
                d.switchFile(int(msg))
                return d, nil
        case toggleFlowMsg:
                d.toggleFlow()
                if w := saveState(d.cfg); w != "" {
                        d.setMsg(w)
                }
                return d, nil
        case backMsg: // from the flow view
                d.open = nil
                return d, nil
        case refreshMsg:
                return d, d.relistFiles(msg.quiet)
        case filesListedMsg:
                if msg.diff != d {
                        return d, nil
                }
                return d, d.filesListed(msg)
        case showMsg:
                d.setMsg(string(msg))
                return d, nil
        case toggleUntrackedMsg:
                if d.gitDiff == nil {
                        d.setMsg(untrackedOnlyInDiff)
                        return d, nil
                }
                d.cfg.Settings.Untracked = !d.cfg.Settings.Untracked // not saved
                return d, d.relistFiles(false)
        }
        if d.flowShown() {
                _, cmd := d.flow.Update(msg)
                if _, file, _ := d.flow.position(); file >= 0 {
                        d.cursor = file
                        d.scrollToCursor()
                }
                return d, cmd
        }
        if d.open != nil {
                if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok && d.cfg.Keys.Key(keys.DiffView, k.String()).Action == "to_files" {
                        d.open.updateStats()
                        d.open = nil
                        return d, nil
                }
                _, cmd := d.open.view.Update(msg)
                return d, cmd
        }
        if k, ok := msg.(tea.KeyPressMsg); ok {
                return d, d.key(k.String())
        }
        return d, nil
}

func (d *Diff) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        d.msg = ""
        cursor := d.cursor
        defer func() {
                if d.cursor != cursor {
                        d.previewFor = nil // another file shows its preview from the start
                }
        }()
        if d.prefix != "" {
                d.prefix = ""
                if isMarkLetter(k) {
                        d.gotoMark(k, (d.height-2)/2)
                }
                return nil
        }
        h := d.cfg.Keys.Key(keys.FileList, k)
        page := max(d.listHeight()-1, 1)
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, page, len(d.files)); ok {
                if h := d.boxHeight(); paging && h > 0 && d.cursor < len(d.files) {
                        // The preview scrolls, by its height less a line, as the list pages.
                        d.scrollPreview(direction(delta > 0) * max(h-3, 1))
                        return nil
                }
                d.moveCursor(delta)
                return nil
        }
        switch h.Action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "open":
                if d.inLog() && d.cfg.Settings.Flow && d.cursor < len(d.files) {
                        file := d.cursor
                        return func() tea.Msg { return flowAtMsg(file) }
                }
                d.openFile()
        case "to_commits":
                if d.inLog() {
                        return func() tea.Msg { return backMsg{} }
                }
        case "history":
                if d.inLog() {
                        return switchCommit(h)
                }
        case "flow":
                return toggleFlow
        case "jump":
                d.prefix, d.msg = h.Action, jumpPrompt
        default:
                return pageKey(d.cfg.Keys, h, false)
        }
        return nil
}

// diffOf returns the diff of e, creating it on first use.
func (d *Diff) diffOf(e *fileEntry) *fileAlign {
        if e.align == nil {
                e.align = newFileAlign(d.cfg, e.paneNames(), [2][]string{e.Old, e.New})
                e.align.markPath = e.Path
        }
        return e.align
}

// viewOf returns the diff view of e, creating it on first use.
func (d *Diff) viewOf(e *fileEntry) *FileDiff {
        if e.view == nil {
                e.view = newFileDiff(d.diffOf(e))
                e.view.canBack = true
                e.view.commitNav = d.inLog()
        }
        return e.view
}

func (d *Diff) openFile() {
        if d.cursor >= len(d.files) {
                return
        }
        if d.cfg.Settings.Flow && !d.inLog() {
                d.showFlow(d.cursor, nil)
                return
        }
        e := d.files[d.cursor]
        if e.Binary {
                d.msg = e.Path + " is a binary file"
                return
        }
        d.viewOf(e).Update(tea.WindowSizeMsg{Width: d.width, Height: d.height})
        d.open = e
}

// showFlow shows the flow view on file, where its diff view from leaves
// it, or on its bar for from nil (see Flow.showFrom).
func (d *Diff) showFlow(file int, from *FileDiff) {
        if d.flow == nil {
                d.flow = flowOfDiff(d)
        }
        d.open = d.files[file]
        d.flow.width, d.flow.height = d.width, d.height
        d.flow.showFrom(0, file, from)
}

// toggleFlow switches between the diff view of the file under the cursor
// and the flow view, keeping the cursor on its line and screen row.
func (d *Diff) toggleFlow() {
        st := &d.cfg.Settings
        if !st.Flow {
                st.Flow = true
                switch {
                case len(d.files) == 0:
                case d.open == nil:
                        d.showFlow(d.cursor, nil)
                default:
                        d.showFlow(d.cursor, d.open.view)
                }
                return
        }
        st.Flow = false
        if d.open == nil {
                return
        }
        _, file, line := d.flow.position()
        screenRow := d.flow.cursor - d.flow.top
        d.open = nil
        if file < 0 {
                return
        }
        d.cursor = file
        d.scrollToCursor()
        if d.files[file].Binary {
                return
        }
        d.openFile()
        d.open.view.takeOver(d.flow, line, screenRow)
}

// gotoMark opens the file of mark letter, when it is in the diff, with the
// cursor on its line at screen row screenRow.
func (d *Diff) gotoMark(letter string, screenRow int) {
        mk, ok := d.cfg.Mark(letter)
        if !ok {
                d.setMsg("no mark " + letter)
                return
        }
        i := d.fileByPath(mk.Path)
        if i < 0 {
                d.setMsg(notInDiff(letter, mk))
                return
        }
        d.openIndex(i)
        if d.flowShown() {
                d.flow.gotoMark(letter)
                return
        }
        if at, ok := d.open.view.markSpot(letter, mk); ok {
                d.open.view.show(at, screenRow)
        }
}

// jump opens the file of j's end of moved code with the cursor on its line.
func (d *Diff) jump(j jumpMsg) {
        i := d.fileByGroup(j.end.file)
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        d.openIndex(i)
        d.open.view.show(d.open.align.spotOf(j.end.side, j.end.line, d.open.view.shape()), j.screenRow)
        d.open.view.msg = j.msg
}

// switchFile opens the next (dir > 0) or previous file that can be shown.
func (d *Diff) switchFile(dir int) {
        for i := d.cursor + dir; i >= 0 && i < len(d.files); i += dir {
                if !d.files[i].Binary {
                        d.openIndex(i)
                        return
                }
        }
        d.setMsg(ends(dir > 0, "last file", "first file"))
}

// scrollPreview scrolls the preview of the file under the cursor by delta
// lines: up to its first line, down until its last one is the last shown.
func (d *Diff) scrollPreview(delta int) {
        e := d.files[d.cursor]
        if e.Binary {
                return
        }
        screen, at := d.diffOf(e).screenLines(d.previewLayout(e).shape)
        from := d.previewStart(e, screen, at)
        if delta > 0 {
                from = min(from+delta, max(len(screen)-(d.boxHeight()-2), from))
        } else {
                from = max(from+delta, 0)
        }
        d.previewFor, d.previewTop = e, from
}

// previewLayout returns the layout of the preview of e: as wide as the
// box's inside, scrolled like the file's diff view.
func (d *Diff) previewLayout(e *fileEntry) layout {
        lo := layout{width: max(d.width-4, 0)}
        lo.shape = newShape(d.cfg, lo.width)
        if e.view != nil {
                lo.xoff, lo.active = e.view.xoff, e.view.active
        }
        return lo
}

// previewStart returns the first of the screen lines of e's diff, indexed by
// at, that its preview shows: where pgup and pgdn scrolled it, else the
// context of the first change.
func (d *Diff) previewStart(e *fileEntry, screen []sline, at map[sline]int) int {
        if d.previewFor == e {
                return max(min(d.previewTop, len(screen)-1), 0)
        }
        a := d.diffOf(e)
        for i := range screen {
                if a.blockStart(screen, i) {
                        // The rows of context before the block, else its first line.
                        return min(at[sline{max(screen[i].v-d.cfg.Settings.Context, 0), 0}], i)
                }
        }
        return 0
}

// preview renders the diff of e in a box height lines high, starting with
// the context of the first change, or where pgup and pgdn scrolled it.
func (d *Diff) preview(e *fileEntry, height int) string {
        t := d.cfg.Theme
        title := t.Heading.Render(" " + e.name() + " ")
        if e.Binary {
                return box(t, d.width, height, title, []string{t.Hint.Render("binary file")})
        }
        a := d.diffOf(e)
        if len(a.view) == 0 { // empty on both sides
                return box(t, d.width, height, title, nil)
        }
        lo := d.previewLayout(e)
        screen, at := a.screenLines(lo.shape)
        from := d.previewStart(e, screen, at)
        var lines []string
        for i := from; i < len(screen) && len(lines) < height-2; i++ {
                lines = append(lines, a.render(screen[i], false, lo))
        }
        return box(t, d.width, height, title, lines)
}

func (d *Diff) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(d.frame()) }

func (d *Diff) frame() string {
        if d.flowShown() {
                return d.flow.frame()
        }
        if d.open != nil {
                return d.open.view.frame()
        }
        if d.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }

        t := d.cfg.Theme
        var added, removed int
        addW, remW, lblW := 1, 1, 0
        for _, e := range d.files {
                added, removed = added+e.added, removed+e.removed
                addW = max(addW, len(fmt.Sprint(e.added)))
                remW = max(remW, len(fmt.Sprint(e.removed)))
                lblW = max(lblW, ansi.StringWidth(e.label(t)))
        }

        // Styles are rendered side by side: lipgloss underlining breaks nested
        // escape sequences.
        heading := t.Heading
        changed := fmt.Sprintf("%d files changed", len(d.files))
        if len(d.files) == 1 {
                changed = "1 file changed"
        }
        head := " " + heading.Render(changed)
        if c := d.commit; c != nil {
                head = " " + t.CommitHash.Render(c.Short) + " " + heading.Render(c.Subject)
        }
        head += "  " + t.Plus.Render(fmt.Sprintf("+%d", added)) + " " + t.Minus.Render(fmt.Sprintf("-%d", removed))
        if c := d.commit; c != nil {
                head += "  " + t.Decoration.Render(c.Author+"  "+c.Date)
        }
        if d.gitDiff != nil {
                cmd := diffCommand(d.diffArgs)
                if d.cfg.Settings.Untracked {
                        cmd += " + untracked"
                }
                head += "  " + t.Hint.Render(printable(cmd))
        }

        item := func(i int, st func(lipgloss.Style) lipgloss.Style) string {
                e := d.files[i]
                cnt := counts(t, st, e.added, e.removed, addW, remW)
                if e.Binary {
                        cnt = st(t.Filler).Render(fmt.Sprintf("%-*s", addW+remW+3, "binary"))
                }
                lbl := st(lipgloss.NewStyle()).Render(" " + pad(e.label(t), lblW, lipgloss.NewStyle()) + " ")
                return lbl + " " + cnt + "  " + st(lipgloss.NewStyle()).Render(e.name())
        }
        k := barKeys{enter: "open", commits: d.inLog(), flow: true}
        if d.inLog() {
                k.back = "to_commits"
        }
        hints := statusKeys(d.cfg, k)
        return d.render(t, head, item, func(h int) string { return d.preview(d.files[d.cursor], h) }, pageStatus(d.cfg, d.msg, hints))
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/settings"
        "jdiff/internal/theme"
)

type side int

const (
        left side = iota
        right
)

func (s side) other() side { return 1 - s }

// lineOf returns the line of side s shown in r, or -1.
func lineOf(r diff.Row, s side) int {
        if s == left {
                return r.Left
        }
        return r.Right
}

// emphOf returns the emphasized parts of the line of side s in row i, none
// unless it is a Change row. They are computed when the row is first drawn
// and kept until the next alignment: computing them for all rows when a
// large change is loaded took seconds.
func (a *fileAlign) emphOf(i int, s side) []diff.Span {
        r := a.rows[i]
        if r.Kind != diff.Change {
                return nil
        }
        e, ok := a.emph[i]
        if !ok {
                e[left], e[right] = diff.Intraline(a.lines[left][r.Left], a.lines[right][r.Right],
                        a.cfg.Settings.OnlyChangedWhitespaces)
                a.emph[i] = e
        }
        return e[s]
}

// anchorLine returns the line of side s that an anchors.
func anchorLine(an diff.Anchor, s side) int {
        if s == left {
                return an.Left
        }
        return an.Right
}

// mark is a line chosen as the first half of a new anchor.
type mark struct {
        side side
        line int
}

// vrow is a displayed row: the diff row row, or when hidden > 0 a fold
// standing for the unchanged rows [row, row+hidden). block is the index of
// the foldable block the row belongs to, or -1.
type vrow struct{ row, hidden, block int }

// sline is a screen line of a diff: line sub of displayed row v. Side by
// side a displayed row is one line, or with wrapping as many as its longer
// line needs; in the normal layout a changed row has the lines of its
// removed line, then of its added line, each as many as it needs, which
// screenLines puts into the old and the new half of their block.
type sline struct{ v, sub int }

// block is a foldable run of unchanged rows [start, end).
type block struct{ start, end int }

// spot is a cursor in a diff: on line sub of displayed row v, in the pane of
// side side.
type spot struct {
        v, sub int
        side   side
}

// shape is what the screen lines of a diff depend on: the normal layout
// when unified, the width of the view when wrap is not 0, at which long
// lines are wrapped instead of cut, and with lineEnd the column of the
// symbol at the end of lines (line_end, one column).
type shape struct {
        unified bool
        wrap    int
        lineEnd bool
        cr      bool // the \r of all \r\n line ends is drawn, see crShown
}

// layout is how a view draws a diff: width columns wide, scrolled by xoff
// columns unless lines are wrapped, with the cursor in the pane of side
// active, in shape.
type layout struct {
        width, xoff int
        active      side
        shape
}

// fileAlign is the diff of one file as the user anchored and folded it. The
// single file view, the flow view and the preview all show the same
// fileAlign, each with its own cursor.
type fileAlign struct {
        cfg   *config.Config
        names [2]string
        lines [2][]string

        anchors []diff.Anchor // sorted by Left, never crossing
        rows    []diff.Row
        rowOf   [2][]int               // line index -> row index, per side
        emph    map[int][2][]diff.Span // emphasized parts by row, see emphOf

        blocks   []block      // foldable blocks of unchanged rows
        foldOpen map[int]bool // open state by the block's first left line
        view     []vrow       // displayed rows
        viewOf   []int        // row index -> view index
        changes  int          // counts the rebuilds of view

        pending *mark        // first half of an anchor being created
        replace *diff.Anchor // anchor waiting for confirmation to replace conflicts

        // The last reload: it replaced the lines of side reloadSide, moved maps
        // their old indices to the new ones, -1 for lines gone. reloads counts
        // the reloads.
        reloads    int
        reloadSide side
        moved      []int

        widths [2][]int // the columns each line takes when drawn, per side, -1 until measured
        // highlighted is the line drawn last, per side, see content.
        highlighted [2]highlightCache

        markPath   string     // the file's path in marks
        aligns     int        // counts the alignments
        group      *moveGroup // the files searched for moved code with this one
        groupIndex int        // the index of this file in group
}

// highlightKey is what the highlight of a line depends on: the line, its row
// in the alignment, the cursor on it, whether it moved, the text searched
// for, the settings and the theme.
type highlightKey struct {
        line, aligns  int
        cursor, moved bool
        search        config.Query
        settings      settings.Settings
        theme         *theme.Theme
}

type highlightCache struct {
        key highlightKey
        out string
        ok  bool
}

func newFileAlign(cfg *config.Config, names [2]string, lines [2][]string) *fileAlign {
        a := &fileAlign{cfg: cfg, names: names, lines: lines, foldOpen: map[int]bool{}}
        a.measure(left)
        a.measure(right)
        a.align()
        return a
}

// measure forgets the widths of the lines of side s, after its lines were
// set: see width.
func (a *fileAlign) measure(s side) {
        a.widths[s] = make([]int, len(a.lines[s]))
        for i := range a.widths[s] {
                a.widths[s][i] = -1
        }
}

// width returns the columns line of side s takes when drawn, without the \r
// of a \r\n line end (see crShown). It is measured on first use: only
// wrapping needs it, and measuring all lines took most of the time loading a
// large change takes.
func (a *fileAlign) width(s side, line int) int {
        if w := a.widths[s][line]; w >= 0 {
                return w
        }
        w := ansi.StringWidth(printable(strings.TrimSuffix(a.lines[s][line], "\r")))
        a.widths[s][line] = w
        return w
}

// crShown reports whether line of side s ends in a \r that is drawn in sh:
// where it changed, or on all lines with sh.cr.
func (a *fileAlign) crShown(s side, line int, sh shape) bool {
        text := a.lines[s][line]
        if !strings.HasSuffix(text, "\r") {
                return false
        }
        if sh.cr {
                return true
        }
        for _, sp := range a.emphOf(a.rowOf[s][line], s) {
                if sp.End == len(text) {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// reload replaces the lines of side s by lines, the file's new content on
// disk. Lines that are unchanged keep their anchors and folds; anchors on
// lines that are gone are dropped and counted. A pending mark or replace
// prompt is cancelled. Views follow their cursor line through moved, so
// they must catch up before the next reload.
func (a *fileAlign) reload(s side, lines []string) (dropped int) {
        moved := lineMap(a.lines[s], lines)
        var anchors []diff.Anchor
        for _, an := range a.anchors {
                n := moved[anchorLine(an, s)]
                if n < 0 {
                        dropped++
                        continue
                }
                if s == left {
                        an.Left = n
                } else {
                        an.Right = n
                }
                anchors = append(anchors, an)
        }
        a.anchors = anchors
        if s == left {
                open := map[int]bool{}
                for line, o := range a.foldOpen {
                        if n := moved[line]; n >= 0 {
                                open[n] = o
                        }
                }
                a.foldOpen = open
        }
        a.pending, a.replace = nil, nil
        a.lines[s] = lines
        a.measure(s)
        a.reloads, a.reloadSide, a.moved = a.reloads+1, s, moved
        a.align()
        return dropped
}

// follow returns the displayed row that now shows a line of r, a row before
// the diff changed: its left line, else its right one, through the last
// reload when reloaded is set. It returns -1 when neither line is left.
func (a *fileAlign) follow(r diff.Row, reloaded bool) int {
        if i := a.rowNow(r, reloaded); i >= 0 {
                return a.viewOf[i]
        }
        return -1
}

// rowAfterReload returns the index of the row that now holds a line of r, a
// row before the last reload, as follow does, or -1.
func (a *fileAlign) rowAfterReload(r diff.Row) int { return a.rowNow(r, true) }

// rowNow returns the index of the row that now holds the left line of r,
// else its right one, through the last reload when reloaded is set, or -1.
func (a *fileAlign) rowNow(r diff.Row, reloaded bool) int {
        for _, s := range []side{left, right} {
                line := lineOf(r, s)
                if line >= 0 && reloaded && s == a.reloadSide {
                        line = a.moved[line]
                }
                if line >= 0 {
                        return a.rowOf[s][line]
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// lineMap maps the indices of before to those of the same lines in after, -1
// for lines that are not in after. It keeps the order of the lines.
func lineMap(before, after []string) []int {
        to := make([]int, len(before))
        for i := range to {
                to[i] = -1
        }
        for _, r := range diff.Align(before, after, nil) {
                if r.Kind == diff.Equal {
                        to[r.Left] = r.Right
                }
        }
        return to
}

// act runs the anchor and fold action (anchor, cancel_mark, fold or
// fold_all) at the cursor at, in the screen lines of sh. It returns the cursor
// afterwards and the message to show, "" for none.
func (a *fileAlign) act(action string, at spot, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        if a.replace != nil {
                an := *a.replace
                a.replace = nil
                switch action {
                case "anchor":
                        for _, c := range diff.Conflicts(a.anchors, an) {
                                a.removeAnchor(c)
                        }
                        return a.addAnchor(an, at.side, sh)
                case "cancel_mark":
                        return at, "replace cancelled"
                }
        }
        switch action {
        case "cancel_mark":
                if a.pending != nil {
                        a.pending = nil
                        return at, "mark cancelled"
                }
        case "anchor":
                return a.enter(at, sh)
        case "fold":
                return a.toggleNearestBlock(at)
        case "fold_all":
                return a.toggleAllBlocks(at)
        }
        return at, ""
}

// markPrompt returns what to do with the pending mark, "" without one: the
// status lines show it until another message, while the mark waits.
func (a *fileAlign) markPrompt() string {
        if a.pending == nil {
                return ""
        }
        return fmt.Sprintf("marked %s%d, pick the matching line (esc cancels)", sideName(a.pending.side), a.pending.line+1)
}

// enter implements the anchor workflow: mark a line, then pick its partner
// in the other pane. Enter on an anchored line removes that anchor. In the
// normal layout the line under the cursor decides the side.
func (a *fileAlign) enter(at spot, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        if sh.unified {
                at.side = a.unifiedSide(at, sh)
        }
        line := a.lineAt(at.side, at.v)
        if line < 0 {
                if at.v < len(a.view) && a.view[at.v].hidden > 0 {
                        return at, "unchanged lines folded, space expands"
                }
                return at, "no line here"
        }
        if an, ok := a.anchorAt(at.side, line); ok {
                a.removeAnchor(an)
                a.align()
                return a.spotOf(at.side, line, sh), "removed anchor " + anchorName(an)
        }
        if a.pending == nil || a.pending.side == at.side {
                if a.pending != nil && a.pending.line == line {
                        a.pending = nil
                        return at, "mark cancelled"
                }
                a.pending = &mark{at.side, line}
                at.side = at.side.other()
                return at, a.markPrompt()
        }

        an := diff.Anchor{Left: a.pending.line, Right: line}
        if at.side == left {
                an = diff.Anchor{Left: line, Right: a.pending.line}
        }
        if conflicts := diff.Conflicts(a.anchors, an); len(conflicts) > 0 {
                names := make([]string, len(conflicts))
                for i, c := range conflicts {
                        names[i] = anchorName(c)
                }
                a.replace = &an
                return at, fmt.Sprintf("%s crosses %s: enter replaces, esc cancels",
                        anchorName(an), strings.Join(names, ", "))
        }
        return a.addAnchor(an, at.side, sh)
}

// addAnchor adds an, picked last on side s, and returns the cursor on its
// row in the screen lines of sh: the line picked last keeps its place, the
// rest aligns around it.
func (a *fileAlign) addAnchor(an diff.Anchor, s side, sh shape) (spot, string) {
        i, _ := slices.BinarySearchFunc(a.anchors, an, func(x, y diff.Anchor) int {
                return x.Left - y.Left
        })
        a.anchors = slices.Insert(a.anchors, i, an)
        a.pending = nil
        a.align()
        return a.spotOf(s, anchorLine(an, s), sh), "anchored " + anchorName(an)
}

// unifiedSide returns the side of the line under the cursor at in the normal
// layout with the screen lines of sh. An unchanged line belongs to both: it
// takes the side opposite a pending mark, else the old side.
func (a *fileAlign) unifiedSide(at spot, sh shape) side {
        if at.v >= len(a.view) || a.view[at.v].hidden > 0 {
                return at.side
        }
        switch r := a.rows[a.view[at.v].row]; {
        case r.Kind == diff.Delete:
                return left
        case r.Kind == diff.Insert:
                return right
        case r.Kind == diff.Change:
                s, _ := a.unifiedPiece(at.v, at.sub, sh)
                return s
        case a.pending != nil:
                return a.pending.side.other()
        }
        return left
}

func (a *fileAlign) removeAnchor(an diff.Anchor) {
        a.anchors = slices.DeleteFunc(a.anchors, func(x diff.Anchor) bool { return x == an })
}

func (a *fileAlign) anchorAt(s side, line int) (diff.Anchor, bool) {
        for _, an := range a.anchors {
                if anchorLine(an, s) == line {
                        return an, true
                }
        }
        return diff.Anchor{}, false
}

// align computes the rows from the lines and anchors, then the displayed
// rows.
func (a *fileAlign) align() {
        a.rows = diff.Align(a.lines[left], a.lines[right], a.anchors)
        a.emph = map[int][2][]diff.Span{}
        for s := range a.rowOf {
                a.rowOf[s] = make([]int, len(a.lines[s]))
        }
        for i, r := range a.rows {
                if r.Left >= 0 {
                        a.rowOf[left][r.Left] = i
                }
                if r.Right >= 0 {
                        a.rowOf[right][r.Right] = i
                }
        }
        a.aligns++
        a.buildView()
}

// buildView computes the displayed rows. Unchanged rows further than the
// context from any change, anchor or pending mark form foldable blocks,
// shown as one fold row while closed; a single such row is never a block.
// The view is built anew, so views holding the old one can still read it.
func (a *fileAlign) buildView() {
        keep := make([]bool, len(a.rows))
        ctx := max(a.cfg.Settings.Context, 0)
        for i, r := range a.rows {
                if r.Kind != diff.Equal || r.Anchored || a.isPending(r) {
                        for j := max(i-ctx, 0); j <= min(i+ctx, len(a.rows)-1); j++ {
                                keep[j] = true
                        }
                }
        }

        a.blocks = nil
        a.view = nil
        a.viewOf = make([]int, len(a.rows))
        for i := 0; i < len(a.rows); {
                j := i + 1
                if !keep[i] {
                        for j < len(a.rows) && !keep[j] {
                                j++
                        }
                }
                if j-i < 2 {
                        a.viewOf[i] = len(a.view)
                        a.view = append(a.view, vrow{row: i, block: -1})
                        i = j
                        continue
                }
                b := len(a.blocks)
                a.blocks = append(a.blocks, block{i, j})
                open := a.blockOpen(a.blocks[b])
                for k := i; k < j; k++ {
                        a.viewOf[k] = len(a.view)
                        if open {
                                a.view = append(a.view, vrow{row: k, block: b})
                        }
                }
                if !open {
                        a.view = append(a.view, vrow{row: i, hidden: j - i, block: b})
                }
                i = j
        }
        a.changes++
}

// newShape returns the shape of the screen lines of a view width columns
// wide, as the settings of cfg ask for.
func newShape(cfg *config.Config, width int) shape {
        sh := shape{unified: !cfg.Settings.SideBySide}
        if cfg.Settings.Wrap {
                sh.wrap, sh.lineEnd, sh.cr = width, cfg.Settings.ShowWhitespaces, cfg.Settings.ShowCR
        }
        return sh
}

// contentWidth returns the columns left for the text of a line in a view
// width columns wide, in the normal layout when unified, else side by side.
func (a *fileAlign) contentWidth(width int, unified bool) int {
        nw := a.numWidth()
        if unified {
                return max(width-2*nw-4, 0)
        }
        return max((width-1)/2-nw-3, 0)
}

// pieces returns the number of screen lines line of side s takes in sh: 1,
// or the pieces it is wrapped into. A missing line takes 1.
func (a *fileAlign) pieces(s side, line int, sh shape) int {
        cw := a.contentWidth(sh.wrap, sh.unified)
        if sh.wrap == 0 || line < 0 || cw == 0 {
                return 1
        }
        w := a.width(s, line)
        if sh.lineEnd {
                w++
        }
        if a.crShown(s, line, sh) {
                w++
        }
        return max((w+cw-1)/cw, 1)
}

// rowLines returns the number of screen lines of displayed row v in sh.
func (a *fileAlign) rowLines(v int, sh shape) int {
        if a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return 1
        }
        r := a.rows[a.view[v].row]
        switch {
        case !sh.unified:
                return max(a.pieces(left, r.Left, sh), a.pieces(right, r.Right, sh))
        case r.Kind == diff.Change:
                return a.pieces(left, r.Left, sh) + a.pieces(right, r.Right, sh)
        }
        s, _ := a.unifiedPiece(v, 0, sh)
        return a.pieces(s, lineOf(r, s), sh)
}

// unifiedPiece returns the side whose line screen line sub of displayed row
// v shows in the normal layout with sh, and which piece of it.
func (a *fileAlign) unifiedPiece(v, sub int, sh shape) (side, int) {
        if a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return right, 0
        }
        switch r := a.rows[a.view[v].row]; r.Kind {
        case diff.Delete:
                return left, sub
        case diff.Change:
                if n := a.pieces(left, r.Left, sh); sub >= n {
                        return right, sub - n
                }
                return left, sub
        }
        return right, sub
}

// firstSub returns the screen line of displayed row v that starts the line
// of side s in sh. Side by side without wrapping it is the one of the normal
// layout: the view clamps it to the row's only line, and it keeps the side
// for switching back.
func (a *fileAlign) firstSub(v int, s side, sh shape) int {
        if !sh.unified && sh.wrap > 0 {
                return 0
        }
        r := a.rows[a.view[v].row]
        if a.view[v].hidden > 0 || r.Kind != diff.Change || s == left {
                return 0
        }
        return a.pieces(left, r.Left, sh)
}

// convertSub returns the screen line of displayed row v in sh that shows
// what screen line sub in from shows: the same piece of the same side's
// line, as far as it has one. Side by side with wrapping, active is the side
// of the cursor. Without wrapping in either shape, sub is kept as it is, as
// it was before lines could wrap.
func (a *fileAlign) convertSub(v, sub int, from, sh shape, active side) int {
        if from.wrap == 0 && sh.wrap == 0 || a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return sub
        }
        s, piece := active, sub
        switch {
        case from.unified:
                s, piece = a.unifiedPiece(v, sub, from)
        case from.wrap == 0:
                s, piece = a.unifiedPiece(v, sub, shape{unified: true})
        }
        if !sh.unified && sh.wrap == 0 {
                return a.firstSub(v, s, sh)
        }
        r := a.rows[a.view[v].row]
        if !sh.unified {
                return min(piece, a.rowLines(v, sh)-1)
        }
        if lineOf(r, s) < 0 || (r.Kind != diff.Change && r.Kind != diff.Equal) {
                s, _ = a.unifiedPiece(v, 0, sh) // the row shows only one side
        }
        return a.firstSub(v, s, sh) + min(piece, a.pieces(s, lineOf(r, s), sh)-1)
}

// screenLines returns the screen lines of the displayed rows in sh and the
// index of each of them. In the normal layout a block of changed rows shows
// all its old lines, then all its new ones, as git diff does; so the two
// lines of a changed row are not next to each other.
func (a *fileAlign) screenLines(sh shape) (screen []sline, at map[sline]int) {
        at = make(map[sline]int, len(a.view))
        add := func(v, sub int) {
                at[sline{v, sub}] = len(screen)
                screen = append(screen, sline{v, sub})
        }
        for v := 0; v < len(a.view); {
                if !sh.unified || !a.changed(v) {
                        for sub := range a.rowLines(v, sh) {
                                add(v, sub)
                        }
                        v++
                        continue
                }
                end := v
                for end < len(a.view) && a.changed(end) {
                        end++
                }
                for _, s := range []side{left, right} {
                        for w := v; w < end; w++ {
                                for sub := range a.rowLines(w, sh) {
                                        if of, _ := a.unifiedPiece(w, sub, sh); of == s {
                                                add(w, sub)
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                }
                v = end
        }
        return screen, at
}

// blockStart reports whether screen line i of screen starts a block of
// changed rows.
func (a *fileAlign) blockStart(screen []sline, i int) bool {
        return a.changed(screen[i].v) && (i == 0 || !a.changed(screen[i-1].v))
}

// blockOpen reports whether b is unfolded. Blocks start closed when
// collapse_unchanged is set; toggled state is kept by the block's first
// line, so it survives realigning as long as the block starts there.
func (a *fileAlign) blockOpen(b block) bool {
        if open, ok := a.foldOpen[a.rows[b.start].Left]; ok {
                return open
        }
        return !a.cfg.Settings.CollapseUnchanged
}

func (a *fileAlign) isPending(r diff.Row) bool {
        return a.pending != nil && lineOf(r, a.pending.side) == a.pending.line
}

// toggleNearestBlock folds or unfolds the block of unchanged rows under the
// cursor at, or else the one closest to it (the lower one on a tie). The
// cursor stays on its row, or on the fold containing it.
func (a *fileAlign) toggleNearestBlock(at spot) (spot, string) {
        b := a.nearestBlock(at.v)
        if b < 0 {
                return at, "no unchanged code to fold"
        }
        r := a.view[at.v].row
        blk := a.blocks[b]
        a.foldOpen[a.rows[blk.start].Left] = !a.blockOpen(blk)
        a.buildView()
        at.v = a.viewOf[r]
        return at, ""
}

// toggleAllBlocks unfolds all blocks of unchanged rows when one of them is
// folded, and folds them all otherwise. The cursor at stays on its row, or
// on the fold containing it.
func (a *fileAlign) toggleAllBlocks(at spot) (spot, string) {
        if len(a.blocks) == 0 {
                return at, "no unchanged code to fold"
        }
        open := false
        for _, b := range a.blocks {
                if !a.blockOpen(b) {
                        open = true
                        break
                }
        }
        r := a.view[at.v].row
        for _, b := range a.blocks {
                a.foldOpen[a.rows[b.start].Left] = open
        }
        a.buildView()
        at.v = a.viewOf[r]
        return at, ""
}

// nearestBlock returns the index of the block at or closest to displayed
// row v, or -1 when there is none.
func (a *fileAlign) nearestBlock(v int) int {
        for dist := 0; dist < len(a.view); dist++ {
                for _, w := range []int{v + dist, v - dist} {
                        if w >= 0 && w < len(a.view) && a.view[w].block >= 0 {
                                return a.view[w].block
                        }
                }
        }
        return -1
}

// lineAt returns the line index shown on side s of displayed row v, or -1.
func (a *fileAlign) lineAt(s side, v int) int {
        if v < 0 || v >= len(a.view) || a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return -1
        }
        return lineOf(a.rows[a.view[v].row], s)
}

// changed reports whether displayed row v is a changed row, not unchanged or
// a fold.
func (a *fileAlign) changed(v int) bool {
        return a.view[v].hidden == 0 && a.rows[a.view[v].row].Kind != diff.Equal
}

func sideName(s side) string {
        if s == left {
                return "L"
        }
        return "R"
}

func anchorName(an diff.Anchor) string {
        return fmt.Sprintf("L%d↔R%d", an.Left+1, an.Right+1)
}

package ui

import (
        "slices"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
        "jdiff/internal/source"
        "jdiff/watch"
)

// FileDiff is the Bubble Tea model of the diff view (keys of diff_view): a
// single file view of a fileAlign, which other views may show and change too.
type FileDiff struct {
        *fileAlign

        screen      []sline       // screen lines of the displayed rows
        screenAt    map[sline]int // screen line -> its index in screen
        screenShape shape         // what screen was built for
        // The displayed rows screen was built for, to find the cursor's line
        // again when another view or a reload changed them.
        screenChanges int
        screenReloads int
        screenView    []vrow
        screenRows    []diff.Row

        cursor int // index into view
        sub    int // line of the cursor row in the normal layout
        active side
        top    int // first screen line
        xoff   int // horizontal scroll in columns

        width, height int
        canBack       bool // shown from the file list, which handles backspace
        commitNav     bool // shown from the log, which switches commits

        msg    string
        onDisk *source.File // the files on disk, reloaded when they change; nil when not watched
        // disk watches them; those it does not watch are checked every
        // watch_interval.
        disk   diskWatch
        prefix string // the action, "set_mark" or "jump", while waiting for the letter of a mark
}

// NewFileDiff returns a model comparing oldLines (left) with newLines (right).
func NewFileDiff(cfg *config.Config, oldName, newName string, oldLines, newLines []string) *FileDiff {
        a := newFileAlign(cfg, [2]string{oldName, newName}, [2][]string{oldLines, newLines})
        a.markPath = newName
        (&moveGroup{}).add(a, a.names)
        return newFileDiff(a)
}

// NewWatchedFileDiff returns a model comparing the sides of f, which it reloads
// when they change on disk (see watch_interval).
func NewWatchedFileDiff(cfg *config.Config, f source.File) *FileDiff {
        m := NewFileDiff(cfg, f.OldFile, f.NewFile, f.Old, f.New)
        m.onDisk = &f
        m.markPath = repoPath(cfg.Repo(), f.NewFile)
        return m
}

// newFileDiff returns the single file view of a.
func newFileDiff(a *fileAlign) *FileDiff {
        m := &FileDiff{fileAlign: a}
        m.buildScreen()
        if slices.Equal(a.lines[left], a.lines[right]) {
                m.msg = "files are identical"
        }
        if w := a.cfg.Warning(); w != "" {
                m.msg = w
        }
        return m
}

// Init starts watching the files, when they are on disk, see watchFiles.
func (m *FileDiff) Init() tea.Cmd { return m.watchFiles() }

// watchFiles watches the files, when they are on disk, while auto reload is
// on, and returns the command waiting for their changes and the check of
// those not watched; off, it watches them no more.
func (m *FileDiff) watchFiles() tea.Cmd {
        if m.onDisk == nil {
                return nil
        }
        if !m.cfg.AutoReload {
                m.disk.stop()
                return nil
        }
        cmd, msg := m.disk.set(m.cfg, diskPaths(*m.onDisk))
        if msg != "" {
                m.msg = msg
        }
        return batch(cmd, m.poll())
}

// shareWatcher makes m watch its files by w, which App shares.
func (m *FileDiff) shareWatcher(w *watch.Watcher) { m.disk.share(w) }

// poll returns the next check of the files, unless one is pending or both
// are watched.
func (m *FileDiff) poll() tea.Cmd {
        if m.onDisk == nil {
                return nil
        }
        return m.disk.poll(m.cfg, m, diskPaths(*m.onDisk))
}

func (m *FileDiff) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        m.syncLayout()
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                m.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        m.width, m.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                        m.syncLayout() // wrapped lines take other pieces
                })
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                return m, m.key(msg.String())
        case autoReloadMsg:
                if msg.on && m.onDisk != nil { // changed while off
                        if msg := refreshFile(m.onDisk, m.fileAlign, m.syncLayout); msg != "" {
                                m.msg = msg
                        }
                }
                return m, m.watchFiles()
        case watchTickMsg:
                if !m.disk.ticked(m.cfg, m, msg) {
                        return m, nil
                }
                if msg := refreshFile(m.onDisk, m.fileAlign, m.syncLayout); msg != "" {
                        m.msg = msg
                }
                return m, m.poll()
        case changedMsg:
                if msg.w == nil || msg.w != m.disk.w {
                        return m, nil
                }
                if msg := refreshFile(m.onDisk, m.fileAlign, m.syncLayout); msg != "" {
                        m.msg = msg
                }
                return m, batch(m.disk.next(), m.poll())
        case watchEndedMsg:
                if !m.disk.ended(msg.w) {
                        return m, nil
                }
                return m, m.poll()
        case refreshMsg:
                m.refresh(msg.quiet)
        case showMsg:
                m.msg = string(msg)
        case toggleUntrackedMsg:
                m.msg = untrackedOnlyInDiff
        case toggleFlowMsg:
                m.msg = flowOnlyOfSeveral
        case settingChangedMsg:
                if msg.problem != "" {
                        m.msg = msg.problem
                }
                if msg.action == "inotify_max_dirs" {
                        m.disk.limit(m.cfg)
                        return m, m.poll()
                }
        case findMsg:
                m.findText(int(msg))
        }
        return m, nil
}

// stop stops watching the files: the view is replaced.
func (m *FileDiff) stop() { m.disk.stop() }

// refresh reads both files again, whether their stamps changed or not. With
// quiet, it does not say when nothing changed.
func (m *FileDiff) refresh(quiet bool) {
        if m.onDisk == nil {
                m.msg = nothingToReload
                return
        }
        m.onDisk.OldStamp, m.onDisk.NewStamp = source.Stamp{}, source.Stamp{}
        switch msg := refreshFile(m.onDisk, m.fileAlign, m.syncLayout); {
        case msg != "":
                m.msg = msg
        case !quiet:
                m.msg = nothingChanged
        }
}

func (m *FileDiff) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        h := m.cfg.Keys.Key(keys.DiffView, k)
        if m.replace != nil && h.Action != "anchor" && h.Action != "cancel_mark" {
                m.replace = nil
        }
        m.msg = ""
        if p := m.prefix; p != "" {
                m.prefix = ""
                switch {
                case !isMarkLetter(k):
                case p == "set_mark":
                        m.msg = setMark(m.cfg, m.fileAlign, k, m.cursor)
                default:
                        return m.gotoMark(k, m.pos()-m.top)
                }
                return nil
        }
        if cmd, ok := diffKey(m, h, max(m.bodyHeight()-1, 1), len(m.screen)); ok {
                return cmd
        }
        switch h.Action {
        case "next_file":
                if m.canBack {
                        dir := switchFileMsg(direction(h.Index == 0))
                        return func() tea.Msg { return dir }
                }
        case "history":
                if m.commitNav {
                        return switchCommit(h)
                }
        case "flow":
                if m.canBack {
                        return toggleFlow
                }
        case "anchor", "cancel_mark", "fold", "fold_all":
                m.act(h.Action)
        case "moved":
                return m.jump()
        default:
                return pageKey(m.cfg.Keys, h, false)
        }
        return nil
}

// scrollSideways, switchPane and askLetter act for diffKey.
func (m *FileDiff) scrollSideways(cols int)      { m.xoff = max(m.xoff+cols, 0) }
func (m *FileDiff) switchPane()                  { m.active = m.active.other() }
func (m *FileDiff) askLetter(action, msg string) { m.prefix, m.msg = action, msg }

// gotoMark moves the cursor to the line of mark letter, at screen row
// screenRow. A mark in another file of the diff is shown by the file
// list.
func (m *FileDiff) gotoMark(letter string, screenRow int) tea.Cmd {
        mk, ok := m.cfg.Mark(letter)
        switch {
        case !ok:
                m.msg = "no mark " + letter
        case mk.Path == m.markPath:
                if at, ok := m.markSpot(letter, mk); ok {
                        m.jumpTo(at)
                }
        case m.canBack:
                j := markJumpMsg{letter, screenRow}
                return func() tea.Msg { return j }
        default:
                m.msg = notInDiff(letter, mk)
        }
        return nil
}

// markSpot returns the cursor on the line of mark mk, called letter, of
// this file, unfolding it, and sets the message. ok is false for an empty
// new file.
func (m *FileDiff) markSpot(letter string, mk config.Mark) (at spot, ok bool) {
        line, note, ok := m.markLine(mk)
        if !ok {
                m.msg = markMsg(letter, "the new file is empty")
                return spot{}, false
        }
        m.msg = markMsg(letter, note)
        return m.reveal(right, line, m.shape()), true
}

// jump moves the cursor to the other end of the moved line under it (see
// jumpTo). An end in another file is shown by the file list, at the
// cursor's screen row.
func (m *FileDiff) jump() tea.Cmd {
        end, msg, ok := m.otherEnd(spot{m.cursor, m.sub, m.active}, m.screenShape)
        if !ok {
                m.msg = msg
                return nil
        }
        if end.file != m.groupIndex {
                j := jumpMsg{end, m.pos() - m.top, msg}
                return func() tea.Msg { return j }
        }
        m.jumpTo(m.spotOf(end.side, end.line, m.screenShape))
        m.msg = msg
        return nil
}

// jumpTo puts the cursor on at, for a jump within this view (g, '): the
// screen stays when at is on it, see jumpTop.
func (m *FileDiff) jumpTo(at spot) {
        m.syncLayout() // a fold opened for at: the cursor keeps its line
        from := m.pos()
        m.active, m.cursor, m.sub = at.side, at.v, at.sub
        m.top = jumpTop(from, m.pos(), m.top, m.bodyHeight(), len(m.screen))
}

// show puts the cursor on at, at screen row screenRow.
func (m *FileDiff) show(at spot, screenRow int) {
        m.active = at.side
        m.place(at.v, at.sub, screenRow)
}

// act runs an anchor or fold action on the diff at the cursor, which stays on
// its screen row.
func (m *FileDiff) act(action string) {
        var msg string
        m.keepScreenRow(func() {
                changes := m.changes
                var at spot
                at, msg = m.fileAlign.act(action, spot{m.cursor, m.sub, m.active}, m.screenShape)
                if m.changes != changes {
                        m.buildScreen()
                }
                m.cursor, m.sub, m.active = at.v, at.sub, at.side
        })
        if msg != "" {
                m.msg = msg
        }
}

// realign recomputes the diff, after its settings changed.
func (m *FileDiff) realign() {
        m.align()
        m.buildScreen()
        m.cursor = min(m.cursor, max(len(m.view)-1, 0))
}

// shape returns the shape of the screen lines for the current layout and
// width.
func (m *FileDiff) shape() shape { return newShape(m.cfg, m.width) }

// buildScreen computes the screen lines of the displayed rows for the
// current layout and width.
func (m *FileDiff) buildScreen() {
        m.screenShape = m.shape()
        m.screen, m.screenAt = m.screenLines(m.screenShape)
        m.screenChanges, m.screenReloads, m.screenView, m.screenRows = m.changes, m.reloads, m.view, m.rows
}

// syncLayout rebuilds the screen lines when another view or a reload changed
// the diff, keeping the cursor on its line, or when the layout was switched,
// here or in another view, or wrapped lines need other pieces, keeping the
// cursor on its line, piece and screen row.
func (m *FileDiff) syncLayout() {
        switch {
        case m.screenChanges != m.changes:
                m.cursor = m.cursorAfterChange()
                m.buildScreen()
                m.scrollToCursor()
        case m.screenShape != m.shape():
                from := m.screenShape
                m.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        m.buildScreen()
                        if m.cursor < len(m.view) {
                                m.sub = m.convertSub(m.cursor, m.sub, from, m.screenShape, m.active)
                        }
                })
        }
}

// cursorAfterChange returns the displayed row that shows the line of the
// cursor's row in the rows screen was built for: its old line, else its new
// one, followed through a reload since.
func (m *FileDiff) cursorAfterChange() int {
        if m.cursor >= len(m.screenView) || len(m.view) == 0 {
                return 0
        }
        if v := m.follow(m.screenRows[m.screenView[m.cursor].row], m.screenReloads != m.reloads); v >= 0 {
                return v
        }
        return min(m.cursor, len(m.view)-1)
}

// pos returns the screen line of the cursor.
func (m *FileDiff) pos() int {
        if m.cursor >= len(m.view) {
                return 0
        }
        return m.screenAt[sline{m.cursor, min(m.sub, m.rowLines(m.cursor, m.screenShape)-1)}]
}

// place puts the cursor on line sub of displayed row v at screen row
// screenRow.
func (m *FileDiff) place(v, sub, screenRow int) {
        m.syncLayout()
        m.cursor, m.sub = v, sub
        m.top = m.pos() - screenRow
        m.scrollToCursor()
}

// takeOver continues where flow f leaves this file: with its pane and
// scrolling, and, unless line is -1, its cursor on diff row line at screen
// row screenRow.
func (m *FileDiff) takeOver(f *Flow, line, screenRow int) {
        m.active, m.xoff = f.active, f.xoff
        if line >= 0 {
                m.place(line, f.cursorSub(), screenRow)
        }
}

// moveCursor moves the cursor by delta screen lines.
func (m *FileDiff) moveCursor(delta int) {
        if len(m.screen) == 0 {
                return
        }
        s := m.screen[max(min(m.pos()+delta, len(m.screen)-1), 0)]
        m.cursor, m.sub = s.v, s.sub
        m.scrollToCursor()
}

// scroll moves the window by delta screen lines, as far as it can, and the
// cursor along, so that the cursor keeps its screen row.
func (m *FileDiff) scroll(delta int) {
        if len(m.screen) == 0 {
                return
        }
        top := m.top
        m.top = max(min(m.top+delta, len(m.screen)-m.bodyHeight()), 0)
        m.moveCursor(m.top - top)
}

// nextChange moves the cursor to the first line of the next (dir > 0) or
// previous block of changed rows.
func (m *FileDiff) nextChange(dir int) {
        for i := m.pos() + dir; i >= 0 && i < len(m.screen); i += dir {
                if m.blockStart(m.screen, i) {
                        m.cursor, m.sub = m.screen[i].v, m.screen[i].sub
                        m.scrollToCursor()
                        return
                }
        }
        m.msg = "no more changes"
}

func (m *FileDiff) bodyHeight() int { return max(m.height-2, 1) }

// keepScreenRow runs f, which may rebuild the view, move the cursor or
// resize, and then scrolls so that the cursor stays on the same screen row.
// Near the start or end of the file it moves as little as possible.
func (m *FileDiff) keepScreenRow(f func()) {
        offset := m.pos() - m.top
        f()
        m.top = m.pos() - offset
        m.scrollToCursor()
}

func (m *FileDiff) scrollToCursor() {
        m.top = scrollWindow(m.pos(), m.top, m.bodyHeight(), len(m.screen))
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/gitsrc"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
)

// flowKind is the kind of a row of the flow view.
type flowKind int

const (
        flowCommit flowKind = iota // a line of a commit's header
        flowFile                   // the bar starting a file
        flowNote                   // a note in place of a diff: binary file, load error
        flowDiff                   // a displayed row of a file's diff view
)

// flowRow is a row of the flow view. part is the index of its part, file
// the index of its file within the part (-1 for rows of the commit), and
// line the line of the commit header or the index into the file's
// displayed rows; sub is the line of a diff row in the normal layout.
type flowRow struct {
        kind                  flowKind
        part, file, line, sub int
}

// flowPart is one commit of the log, or all files outside the log.
type flowPart struct {
        commit *commitEntry // nil outside the log
        full   []string     // commit header lines with the whole message, the first one is the bar
        short  []string     // commit header lines with the subject only
        diff   *Diff        // the files, nil until loaded
        err    string       // why the files could not be loaded
}

// far moves the cursor to the first or last row.
const far = 1 << 30

// Flow is the Bubble Tea model of the flow view (keys of flow_view): the
// diffs of all files one after the other, each starting with a bar, and in
// the log each commit starting with its message. Files share their diffs with the single file
// view, so anchors and folds carry over; cursor and scrolling are the
// flow's own. In the log, commits load in the background once they are on
// the screen, one at a time; until then a note says that they load.
type Flow struct {
        cfg   *config.Config
        parts []*flowPart
        // load returns the command loading the files of the commit of part,
        // whose result the log gives back to partLoaded; nil outside the log.
        load  func(part int) tea.Cmd
        order historyOrder // of the commits, the parts in the log

        loading *flowPart // the commit loading, nil for none
        ahead   int       // the way of the last move, whose commits a screen beyond load too
        atEnd   bool      // end was pressed last: the cursor stays on the last row
        waiting *flowWait // a search waiting for a commit to load

        rows      []flowRow
        rowAt     map[flowRow]int // index of each row, see find
        stale     bool            // a row changed since rowAt was built
        built     shape           // what the rows of the diffs were built for
        cursor    int
        top       int
        xoff      int
        active    side
        marked    *fileAlign // diff holding a pending mark
        replacing *fileAlign // diff waiting for confirmation to replace anchors
        prefix    string     // the action, "set_mark" or "jump", while waiting for the letter of a mark

        width, height int
        msg           string
}

// flowWait is a search that reached a commit not loaded yet: redo runs it
// again once part is loaded.
type flowWait struct {
        part *flowPart
        redo func()
}

func newFlow(cfg *config.Config, parts []*flowPart, load func(int) tea.Cmd) *Flow {
        f := &Flow{cfg: cfg, parts: parts, load: load}
        f.build()
        return f
}

// flowOfDiff returns the flow view of the files of d, outside the log.
func flowOfDiff(d *Diff) *Flow { return newFlow(d.cfg, []*flowPart{{diff: d}}, nil) }

// newCommitPart returns the part of the flow view for commit e.
func newCommitPart(e *commitEntry) *flowPart {
        return &flowPart{commit: e, full: commitHeader(e.Commit, true), short: commitHeader(e.Commit, false)}
}

// commitHeader returns the header lines of c: the bar, then the message, or
// with full unset its subject only, indented as git log does.
func commitHeader(c gitsrc.Commit, full bool) []string {
        h := []string{fmt.Sprintf("commit %s · %s · %s", c.Short, c.Author, c.Date), "", "    " + c.Subject}
        if full && c.Body != "" {
                h = append(h, "")
                for l := range strings.SplitSeq(c.Body, "\n") {
                        h = append(h, strings.TrimRight("    "+l, " "))
                }
        }
        return append(h, "")
}

func (f *Flow) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

// commitLines returns the header lines of part as currently shown.
func (f *Flow) commitLines(part *flowPart) []string {
        if f.cfg.Settings.FullMessages {
                return part.full
        }
        return part.short
}

func (f *Flow) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        var cmd tea.Cmd
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        f.width, f.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                        f.relocate() // wrapped lines take other pieces
                })
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                cmd = f.key(msg.String())
        case findMsg:
                f.findText(int(msg))
        case settingChangedMsg:
                // Wrapped lines take other pieces, and end in line_end or ␍, which take
                // a column.
                f.keepScreenRow(f.relocate)
                if msg.action == "stay_in_commit" && f.inLog() && f.cfg.Settings.StayInCommit {
                        f.msg = "staying in commit " + f.parts[f.rows[f.cursor].part].commit.Short
                }
                if msg.problem != "" {
                        f.msg = msg.problem
                }
        }
        // A key's own command comes first, alone, as the tests run commands one
        // at a time; the next message starts the load.
        if cmd == nil {
                cmd = f.nextLoad()
        }
        return f, cmd
}

func (f *Flow) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        h := f.cfg.Keys.Key(keys.FlowView, k)
        if f.replacing != nil && h.Action != "anchor" && h.Action != "cancel_mark" {
                f.replacing.replace = nil
                f.replacing = nil
        }
        f.msg = ""
        f.atEnd, f.waiting = false, nil // another key: end and searches let go
        if p := f.prefix; p != "" {
                f.prefix = ""
                switch {
                case !isMarkLetter(k):
                case p == "set_mark":
                        f.setMark(k)
                default:
                        f.gotoMark(k)
                }
                return nil
        }
        if cmd, ok := diffKey(f, h, max(f.bodyHeight()-1, 1), far); ok {
                return cmd
        }
        switch h.Action {
        case "flow":
                return toggleFlow
        case "to_files":
                return func() tea.Msg { return backMsg{} }
        case "next_file":
                f.nextFile(direction(h.Index == 0))
        case "history":
                if !f.inLog() {
                        break
                }
                // The bars of all commits are there, loaded or not: no waiting.
                if r, _ := f.search(f.order.step(historyDir(h)), f.isKind(flowCommit, 0), false, false); r == found {
                        f.barToTop()
                } else {
                        f.msg = ends(historyDir(h) > 0, "last commit", "first commit")
                }
        case "anchor", "cancel_mark", "fold", "fold_all":
                f.delegate(h.Action)
        case "moved":
                f.jump()
        default:
                return pageKey(f.cfg.Keys, h, f.inLog())
        }
        return nil
}

// scrollSideways, switchPane and askLetter act for diffKey.
func (f *Flow) scrollSideways(cols int)      { f.xoff = max(f.xoff+cols, 0) }
func (f *Flow) switchPane()                  { f.active = f.active.other() }
func (f *Flow) askLetter(action, msg string) { f.prefix, f.msg = action, msg }

// delegate runs an anchor or fold action on the diff of the file under the
// cursor.
func (f *Flow) delegate(action string) {
        if action == "cancel_mark" && f.replacing == nil && f.marked != nil && f.marked.pending != nil {
                // The mark may be in another file than the cursor.
                f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                        f.dropMark()
                        f.relocate()
                })
                f.msg = "mark cancelled"
                return
        }
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                if action != "cancel_mark" {
                        f.msg = "no line here"
                }
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        e := f.parts[r.part].diff.files[r.file]
        a := e.align
        if action == "anchor" && f.marked != a {
                f.dropMark()
        }
        var at spot
        f.keepScreenRow(func() {
                at, f.msg = a.act(action, spot{r.line, r.sub, f.active}, f.built)
                e.updateStats()
                f.build()
                if i := f.findDiff(r.part, r.file, at.v, at.sub); i >= 0 {
                        f.cursor = i
                }
        })
        f.active = at.side
        f.replacing = nil
        if a.replace != nil {
                f.replacing = a
        }
        if a.pending != nil {
                f.marked = a
        } else if f.marked == a {
                f.marked = nil
        }
}

// jump moves the cursor to the other end of the moved line under it, in this
// or another file of the commit (see jumpTo).
func (f *Flow) jump() {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                f.msg = "not moved"
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        d := f.parts[r.part].diff
        a := d.files[r.file].align
        end, msg, ok := a.otherEnd(spot{r.line, r.sub, f.active}, f.built)
        f.msg = msg
        if !ok {
                return
        }
        i := d.fileByGroup(end.file)
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        at := d.files[i].align.spotOf(end.side, end.line, f.built)
        f.jumpTo(f.findDiff(r.part, i, at.v, at.sub))
        f.active = at.side
}

// jumpTo moves the cursor to row i, unless it is -1, for a jump within the
// flow (g, '): the screen stays when i is on it, see jumpTop.
func (f *Flow) jumpTo(i int) {
        if i < 0 {
                return
        }
        from := f.cursor
        f.cursor = i
        f.top = jumpTop(from, i, f.top, f.bodyHeight(), len(f.rows))
}

// setMark sets jump mark letter on the new-file line under the cursor.
func (f *Flow) setMark(letter string) {
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.rows[f.cursor].kind != flowDiff {
                f.msg = "no line of the new file here"
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        f.msg = setMark(f.cfg, f.parts[r.part].diff.files[r.file].align, letter, r.line)
}

// gotoMark moves the cursor to the line of jump mark letter when its file is
// in the commit under the cursor, or outside the log among the files (see
// jumpTo).
func (f *Flow) gotoMark(letter string) {
        mk, ok := f.cfg.Mark(letter)
        if !ok {
                f.msg = "no mark " + letter
                return
        }
        if len(f.rows) == 0 || f.parts[f.rows[f.cursor].part].diff == nil {
                f.msg = notInDiff(letter, mk)
                return
        }
        r := f.rows[f.cursor]
        d := f.parts[r.part].diff
        i := d.fileByPath(mk.Path)
        if i < 0 {
                f.msg = notInDiff(letter, mk)
                return
        }
        a := d.files[i].align
        line, note, ok := a.markLine(mk)
        if !ok {
                f.msg = markMsg(letter, "the new file is empty")
                return
        }
        at := a.reveal(right, line, f.built)
        f.relocate() // a fold may have opened: the cursor keeps its row
        f.jumpTo(f.findDiff(r.part, i, at.v, at.sub))
        f.active, f.msg = right, markMsg(letter, note)
}

// dropMark cancels a pending mark; the caller rebuilds the rows.
func (f *Flow) dropMark() {
        if a := f.marked; a != nil && a.pending != nil {
                a.pending = nil
                a.buildView()
        }
        f.marked = nil
}

// build computes the rows from the parts.
func (f *Flow) build() {
        f.rows = f.rows[:0]
        f.built = newShape(f.cfg, f.width)
