                w := ansi.StringWidth(s.text)
                if col+w > from && col < to {
                        s.text = ansi.Cut(s.text, max(from-col, 0), min(to-col, w))
                        out = append(out, s)
                }
                col += w
        }
        return out
}

// cutSegs returns segs cut to width columns, the last of them », when they
// are wider.
func cutSegs(segs []docSeg, width int) []docSeg {
        if segsWidth(segs) <= width {
                return segs
        }
        return append(sliceSegs(segs, 0, width-1), docSeg{"»", docOverflow, -1})
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "path/filepath"
        "strings"
        "unicode/utf8"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
)

// markJumpMsg asks the file list to show mark letter in another file of
// the diff, at screen row screenRow.
type markJumpMsg struct {
        letter    string
        screenRow int
}

// isMarkLetter reports whether k names a mark: a letter a-z or A-Z.
func isMarkLetter(k string) bool {
        if utf8.RuneCountInString(k) != 1 {
                return false
        }
        c := k[0]
        return 'a' <= c && c <= 'z' || 'A' <= c && c <= 'Z'
}

// repoPath returns the path of file in marks: relative to the repository
// repo when inside it, else absolute.
func repoPath(repo, file string) string {
        abs, err := filepath.Abs(file)
        if err != nil {
                return file
        }
        if real, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(abs); err == nil {
                abs = real
        }
        if repo != "" {
                if rel, err := filepath.Rel(repo, abs); err == nil && filepath.IsLocal(rel) {
                        return filepath.ToSlash(rel)
                }
        }
        return abs
}

// setMark sets mark letter on the new-file line of displayed row v of a and
// returns the message to show.
func setMark(cfg *config.Config, a *fileAlign, letter string, v int) string {
        line := a.lineAt(right, v)
        if line < 0 {
                return "no line of the new file here"
        }
        m := config.Mark{Path: a.markPath, Line: line + 1, Text: strings.TrimSuffix(a.lines[right][line], "\r")}
        if err := cfg.SetMark(letter, m); err != nil {
                return "marks: " + err.Error()
        }
        return fmt.Sprintf("mark %s: %s:%d", letter, m.Path, m.Line)
}

// markLine returns the new-file line of mark m in a: its line when it still
// holds its text, else the nearest line that does, else its line number
// within the file, with a note. ok is false for an empty new file.
func (a *fileAlign) markLine(m config.Mark) (line int, note string, ok bool) {
        lines := a.lines[right]
        if len(lines) == 0 {
                return 0, "", false
        }
        want := m.Line - 1
        // Out from the old line until both directions left the file, which may
        // have shrunk far below it.
        for dist := 0; want-dist >= 0 || want+dist < len(lines); dist++ {
                for _, i := range []int{want - dist, want + dist} {
                        // Without the \r of a \r\n line end, which may have changed.
                        if i >= 0 && i < len(lines) && strings.TrimSuffix(lines[i], "\r") == strings.TrimSuffix(m.Text, "\r") {
                                return i, "", true
                        }
                }
        }
        return max(min(want, len(lines)-1), 0), "line changed", true
}

// reveal returns the cursor on line of side s of a in the screen lines of
// sh, unfolding the block of unchanged rows the line is in.
func (a *fileAlign) reveal(s side, line int, sh shape) spot {
        row := a.rowOf[s][line]
        for _, b := range a.blocks {
                if row >= b.start && row < b.end && !a.blockOpen(b) {
                        a.foldOpen[a.rows[b.start].Left] = true
                        a.buildView()
                        break
                }
        }
        return a.spotOf(s, line, sh)
}

// markMsg returns the message for having jumped to mark letter.
func markMsg(letter, note string) string {
        if note != "" {
                return "mark " + letter + ": " + note
        }
        return "mark " + letter
}

// notInDiff returns the message for mark letter m outside the diff shown.
func notInDiff(letter string, m config.Mark) string {
        return fmt.Sprintf("mark %s: %s is not in this diff", letter, m.Path)
}

// MarksPopup wraps the top-level model and shows the jump marks of the
// repository in a popup over it on \: enter jumps to the selected mark, d or
// del deletes it.
type MarksPopup struct {
        tea.Model
        cfg  *config.Config
        open bool
        listPopup
        msg string // a problem saving the marks
}

// WithMarks returns m with the marks popup.
func WithMarks(cfg *config.Config, m tea.Model) *MarksPopup { return &MarksPopup{Model: m, cfg: cfg} }

func (p *MarksPopup) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                p.width, p.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                p.move(0)
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                if cmd, ok := popupKeys(p.cfg.Keys, msg, p.open, "marks", p.key); ok {
                        return p, cmd
                }
        }
        _, cmd := p.Model.Update(msg)
        return p, cmd
}

// key handles k in the popup, or opens it on \\.
func (p *MarksPopup) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        if !p.open {
                p.open, p.cursor, p.top, p.msg = true, 0, 0, ""
                return nil
        }
        if cmd, ok := popupNav(p.cfg.Keys, k, "marks", &p.open, p.move); ok {
                return cmd
        }
        marks := p.cfg.Marks()
        switch k {
        case "d", "delete":
                if p.cursor < len(marks) {
                        if err := p.cfg.DeleteMark(marks[p.cursor].Letter); err != nil {
                                p.msg = "marks: " + err.Error()
                        }
                        p.move(0)
                }
        case "enter":
                if p.cursor < len(marks) {
                        // Jump as the key of jump, ', and the letter do on the page.
                        p.open = false
                        p.Model.Update(runeKey(keys.Name(p.cfg.Keys.Row("jump").Keys[0])))
                        _, cmd := p.Model.Update(runeKey(marks[p.cursor].Letter))
                        return cmd
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// boxHeight is the height of the popup.
func (p *MarksPopup) boxHeight() int { return popupHeight(len(p.cfg.Marks()), p.height) }

func (p *MarksPopup) move(delta int) { p.moveIn(delta, len(p.cfg.Marks())) }

func (p *MarksPopup) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(p.frame()) }

func (p *MarksPopup) frame() string {
        base := p.Model.View().Content
        if !p.open || p.width == 0 {
                return base
        }
        t := p.cfg.Theme
        marks := p.cfg.Marks()
        all := make([]string, len(marks))
        for i, mk := range marks {
                all[i] = fmt.Sprintf(" %s  %s:%d  %s ", mk.Letter, printable(mk.Path), mk.Line, printable(strings.TrimSpace(mk.Text)))
        }
        title := popupTitle(t, "marks", p.msg, "enter jump · d delete · esc close")
        return p.draw(t, base, title, all, "no marks: m and a letter sets one")
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strings"
        "unicode/utf8"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
)

// switchFileMsg asks the file list to show the next (1) or previous (-1)
// file.
type switchFileMsg int

// switchCommitMsg asks the log to show the next (1) or previous (-1) commit
// in history.
type switchCommitMsg int

// backMsg asks the log to return to the commit list, or from its flow view
// to the file list of the commit; or the file list to its list when it
// shows the flow view.
type backMsg struct{}

// flowAtMsg asks the log to show its flow view on the file with this index
// of the commit whose files it shows: enter in that file list in flow mode.
type flowAtMsg int

// jumpMsg asks the file list to show the line of the other end of moved
// code in another file: end, in the file of the group with index end.file,
// at screen row screenRow, with message msg.
type jumpMsg struct {
        end       moveEnd
        screenRow int
        msg       string
}

// refreshMsg asks the top-level view to load what it shows again: run git
// diff or git log again, list the directories again or read the two files.
// With quiet, as auto reload asks, it does not say when nothing changed.
type refreshMsg struct{ quiet bool }

func refreshAll() tea.Msg { return refreshMsg{} }

// nothingChanged is the message after loading again the same.
const nothingChanged = "nothing changed"

// The messages of loading again: nothingToReload where nothing is shown that
// could be, reloading while it runs in the background.
const (
        nothingToReload = "nothing to load again"
        reloading       = "loading again…"
)

// The prompts of the keys of marks, until the letter is pressed.
const (
        setMarkPrompt = "set mark: press a letter"
        jumpPrompt    = "jump to mark: press its letter"
)

// relistState is the state of listing again in the background, which Diff
// (its files) and Log (its commits) must keep the same way: while a listing
// runs, another one is not started, but run once it is done, quietly only
// when all that asked for it meanwhile asked quietly.
type relistState struct {
        running, again, againQuiet bool
}

// start returns whether a listing with quiet starts now; while one runs, it
// notes the listing for after it instead.
func (r *relistState) start(quiet bool) bool {
        if r.running {
                r.againQuiet = quiet && (!r.again || r.againQuiet)
                r.again = true
                return false
        }
        r.running = true
        return true
}

// done ends the listing that ran. It returns whether another one was asked
// for meanwhile, and whether quietly.
func (r *relistState) done() (again, quiet bool) {
        r.running = false
        again, r.again = r.again, false
        return again, r.againQuiet
}

// askAutoReloadMsg asks App to toggle auto reload.
type askAutoReloadMsg struct{}

func askAutoReload() tea.Msg { return askAutoReloadMsg{} }

// showMsg asks the top-level view to show a message in its status bar.
type showMsg string

// refreshed returns the message after loading again: what was added,
// removed and changed, and the anchors dropped from changed files.
func refreshed(added, gone, changed, dropped int) string {
        var parts []string
        for _, c := range []struct {
                n    int
                what string
        }{{added, "new"}, {gone, "gone"}, {changed, "changed"}} {
                if c.n > 0 {
                        parts = append(parts, fmt.Sprintf("%d %s", c.n, c.what))
                }
        }
        switch {
        case dropped == 1:
                parts = append(parts, "1 anchor dropped")
        case dropped > 1:
                parts = append(parts, fmt.Sprintf("%d anchors dropped", dropped))
        }
        if len(parts) == 0 {
                return nothingChanged
        }
        return strings.Join(parts, ", ")
}

// askDiffMsg asks App for the arguments of git diff to show next.
type askDiffMsg struct{}

func askDiff() tea.Msg { return askDiffMsg{} }

// askSettingsMsg asks SettingsPopup to open.
type askSettingsMsg struct{}

func askSettings() tea.Msg { return askSettingsMsg{} }

// toggleSettingMsg asks SettingsPopup to toggle the option of action, with the
// popup closed.
type toggleSettingMsg struct{ action string }

// settingKey returns the command toggling the option of action, nil when no
// option has it or, outside the log's flow view (logFlow), one that only
// matters there.
func settingKey(km *keys.Map, action string, logFlow bool) tea.Cmd {
        options := settingWords(km, settingTable)
        i := slices.IndexFunc(options, func(o settingOption) bool { return o.key != "" && o.action == action })
        if i < 0 || options[i].logFlow && !logFlow {
                return nil
        }
        return func() tea.Msg { return toggleSettingMsg{action} }
}

// pageKey returns the command of key h that all pages hand on: loading again,
// auto reload, the popups of git diff and the settings, or the option of its
// action, see settingKey; nil when h is none of them. The pages hand them on,
// and not the popups themselves as the help and the marks: o is a mark letter
// too, which m and ' take before the page looks at the key.
func pageKey(km *keys.Map, h keys.Hit, logFlow bool) tea.Cmd {
        switch h.Action {
        case "reload":
                return refreshAll
        case "auto_reload":
                return askAutoReload
        case "git_diff":
                return askDiff
        case "settings":
                return askSettings
        }
        return settingKey(km, h.Action, logFlow)
}

// settingChangedMsg tells the page below SettingsPopup that a toggle or a
// number changed, so that it lays out what depends on it. The diff view does
// on any message. problem says why it is not saved, for the page to show when
// the popup is closed.
type settingChangedMsg struct{ action, problem string }

// toggleUntrackedMsg asks the top-level view to toggle the untracked files of
// its git diff, which only the file list of one has.
type toggleUntrackedMsg struct{}

func toggleUntracked() tea.Msg { return toggleUntrackedMsg{} }

// untrackedOnlyInDiff is the answer of the other views.
const untrackedOnlyInDiff = "untracked files: only in jdiff diff"

// flowOnlyOfSeveral is the answer of the diff view of two files.
const flowOnlyOfSeveral = "flow: only with a file list or the log"

// toggleFlowMsg asks the log or the file list to switch between the diff
// view of one file and the flow view of all files.
type toggleFlowMsg struct{}

func toggleFlow() tea.Msg { return toggleFlowMsg{} }

// firstLine returns the first line of err: git writes errors of several lines,
// where the status bar and notes have one.
func firstLine(err error) string {
        line, _, _ := strings.Cut(err.Error(), "\n")
        return line
}

// saveState writes the toggles to state.toml, returning a message when that
// fails, else "".
func saveState(cfg *config.Config) string {
        if err := cfg.SaveState(); err != nil {
                return "state: " + err.Error()
        }
        return ""
}

// The direction in history is decided here only: historyDir turns a key
// into it, historyOrder.step into a step through a list of commits.

// historyDir returns where key h of history goes: 1 to the next commit, for
// the first key of its row, as N and n, -1 to the previous one, as P and p.
func historyDir(h keys.Hit) int { return direction(h.Index == 0) }

// historyOrder is the order of a list of commits, as git log lists them:
// the step from a commit to the next one in history, -1 when the newest
// comes first, as by default, 1 when the oldest does, with --reverse.
type historyOrder int

// orderOf returns the order of a list of commits, the oldest first or not.
func orderOf(oldestFirst bool) historyOrder {
        if oldestFirst {
                return 1
        }
        return -1
}

// step returns the step through the list to go in direction dir in history.
func (o historyOrder) step(dir int) int { return dir * int(o) }

// switchCommit returns the command asking the log for the commit key h goes
// to in history.
func switchCommit(h keys.Hit) tea.Cmd {
        dir := switchCommitMsg(historyDir(h))
        return func() tea.Msg { return dir }
}

// toggle flips setting, one of the toggles of cfg, and saves them, returning
// a message when that fails, else "".
func toggle(cfg *config.Config, setting *bool) string {
        *setting = !*setting
        return saveState(cfg)
}

// runeKey returns the key press typing the character s.
func runeKey(s string) tea.KeyPressMsg {
        r, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s)
        return tea.KeyPressMsg{Code: r, Text: s}
}

// motion returns how many rows the cursor moves on key h, page rows for a
// page and far to the first or last row, whether h pages, so that views of
// file content move the window along, and whether h moves the cursor at all.
func motion(h keys.Hit, page, far int) (delta int, paging, ok bool) {
        switch h.Action {
        case "move":
                return direction(h.Index == 0), false, true
        case "page":
                return direction(h.Index == 1) * page, true, true
        case "ends":
                return direction(h.Index == 1) * far, false, true
        }
        return 0, false, false
}

// diffKeyView is what the keys that work alike in the diff view and the flow
// view act on, see diffKey.
type diffKeyView interface {
        moveCursor(delta int)
        scroll(delta int)        // up and down, the window along
        scrollSideways(cols int) // the lines, not before their start
        switchPane()
        nextChange(dir int)
        findText(dir int)
        askLetter(action, msg string) // the prefix and its message, until the mark's letter
}

// diffKey handles the keys that must work alike in the diff view and the
// flow view, and reports whether h was one of them: motion, quit, pane,
// half_page, scroll, change, search, match, set_mark and jump. page is the
// paging step, far the move to the first or last row. Moving between files
// and commits, anchors, folds and moved lines differ, and stay with each
// view.
func diffKey(v diffKeyView, h keys.Hit, page, far int) (tea.Cmd, bool) {
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, page, far); ok {
                if paging {
                        v.scroll(delta)
                } else {
                        v.moveCursor(delta)
                }
                return nil, true
        }
        switch h.Action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit, true
        case "pane":
                v.switchPane()
        case "half_page":
                v.scroll(halfPage(h, page))
        case "scroll":
                v.scrollSideways(direction(h.Index == 1) * scrollStep)
        case "change":
                v.nextChange(direction(h.Index == 0))
        case "search":
                return askSearch, true
        case "match":
                v.findText(direction(h.Index == 0))
        case "set_mark":
                v.askLetter(h.Action, setMarkPrompt)
        case "jump":
                v.askLetter(h.Action, jumpPrompt)
        default:
                return nil, false
        }
        return nil, true
}

// scrollStep is how many columns the scroll keys move the lines sideways,
// in the diff view and the flow view alike.
const scrollStep = 8

// halfPage returns how many rows half_page key h scrolls, half of page rows,
// the paging step, in the diff view, the flow view and the readme alike.
func halfPage(h keys.Hit, page int) int { return direction(h.Index == 1) * (page / 2) }

func direction(forward bool) int {
        if forward {
                return 1
        }
        return -1
}

// ends returns the message for having reached the last or first item.
func ends(forward bool, last, first string) string {
        if forward {
                return last
        }
        return first
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"

        "jdiff/internal/diff"
)

// moveGroup is the files searched together for moved code: the files of one
// commit or of a comparison of two directories, or the one file of a
// comparison of two files. Never files of different commits.
type moveGroup struct {
        diffs  []*fileAlign
        names  [][2]string // the names of the sides of the files, for messages
        aligns []int       // the alignments the moves were found for
        moves  diff.Moves
}

// add adds a, whose sides are called names, to the group.
func (g *moveGroup) add(a *fileAlign, names [2]string) {
        a.group, a.groupIndex = g, len(g.diffs)
        g.diffs = append(g.diffs, a)
        g.names = append(g.names, names)
        g.aligns = nil
}

// ensure finds the moves again when a diff of the group was aligned anew,
// after an anchor was added or removed or the file was reloaded.
func (g *moveGroup) ensure() {
        fresh := len(g.aligns) == len(g.diffs)
        for i, a := range g.diffs {
                fresh = fresh && g.aligns[i] == a.aligns
        }
        if fresh {
                return
        }
        in := make([]diff.MoveInput, len(g.diffs))
        g.aligns = make([]int, len(g.diffs))
        for i, a := range g.diffs {
                in[i] = diff.MoveInput{Old: a.lines[left], New: a.lines[right], Rows: a.rows}
                g.aligns[i] = a.aligns
        }
        g.moves = diff.FindMoves(in)
}

// movedTo returns where line of side s of a moved: for a removed line the
// line it moved to, for an added line the line it came from.
func (a *fileAlign) movedTo(s side, line int) (diff.Ref, bool) {
        if a.group == nil || line < 0 {
                return diff.Ref{}, false
        }
        a.group.ensure()
        moves := a.group.moves.To
        if s == left {
                moves = a.group.moves.From
        }
        r, ok := moves[a.groupIndex][line]
        return r, ok
}

// moveEnd is the other end of a moved line: line of side side of the file
// at index file of the group.
type moveEnd struct {
        file int
        side side
        line int
}

// otherEnd returns the other end of the moved line under the cursor at, in
// the screen lines of sh, and the message to show there, which says where
// the cursor came from: side by side the line of the active pane, else the
// one beside it. ok is false when neither moved.
func (a *fileAlign) otherEnd(at spot, sh shape) (end moveEnd, msg string, ok bool) {
        s := at.side
        if sh.unified {
                s = a.unifiedSide(at, sh)
        }
        line := a.lineAt(s, at.v)
        r, ok := a.movedTo(s, line)
        if !ok && !sh.unified {
                // Side by side the pane is for anchors: a moved line has no line
                // beside it, so the cursor may be in either pane.
                s = s.other()
                line = a.lineAt(s, at.v)
                r, ok = a.movedTo(s, line)
        }
        if !ok {
                return moveEnd{}, "not moved", false
        }
        how := "moved from"
        if s == right {
                how = "moved to"
        }
        return moveEnd{r.File, s.other(), r.Line}, fmt.Sprintf("%s %s:%d", how, a.group.names[a.groupIndex][s], line+1), true
}

// spotOf returns the cursor on the start of line of side s of a in the
// screen lines of sh (see firstSub).
func (a *fileAlign) spotOf(s side, line int, sh shape) spot {
        v := a.viewOf[a.rowOf[s][line]]
        return spot{v, a.firstSub(v, s, sh), s}
}

package ui

import (
        "cmp"
        "fmt"
        "io/fs"
        "path"
        "slices"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
)

// Readme shows the pages of jdiff's documentation, markdown rendered for the
// terminal: jdiff --readme (keys of doc_view). A cursor line moves through a
// page; enter follows the link it is on, the first unless h l or tab chose
// another, to another page or heading, backspace goes back. Only links that lead to a file of
// docs, and a heading there, or to the web are links. A file that is no
// markdown, reached by a link, shows as it is.
type Readme struct {
        cfg      *config.Config
        docs     fs.FS
        order    []string                  // the pages in docs, in the order of n and p
        page     string                    // the file of docs shown
        anchors  map[string]map[string]int // the headings of other pages, see anchorsOf
        rendered docPage
        forWidth int // the width rendered is for, 0 before the first
        top      int // the first line shown
        cursor   int // the line of the cursor
        link     int // the selected link, on the cursor's line; -1 for none
        back     []docPlace
        msg      string
        width    int
        height   int
}

// docPlace is where a link was followed from, and the link.
type docPlace struct {
        page              string
        top, cursor, link int
}

// NewReadme returns the documentation in docs, whose pages are order, on page
// order[page].
func NewReadme(cfg *config.Config, docs fs.FS, order []string, page int) *Readme {
        return &Readme{cfg: cfg, docs: docs, order: order, page: order[page], anchors: map[string]map[string]int{}, link: -1}
}

func (r *Readme) Init() tea.Cmd { return nil }

func (r *Readme) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                r.width, r.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                r.render()
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                return r, r.key(msg.String())
        case findMsg:
                r.findText(int(msg))
        }
        return r, nil
}

// textWidth is the width pages are rendered for: the screen but a column
// on each side, the left one for the cursor.
func (r *Readme) textWidth() int { return max(r.width-2, 1) }

// bodyHeight is the number of lines shown, between the title and the status
// bar.
func (r *Readme) bodyHeight() int { return max(r.height-2, 1) }

// render renders the page for the width of the screen, unless it is already.
func (r *Readme) render() {
        if r.forWidth == r.textWidth() {
                return
        }
        src, err := fs.ReadFile(r.docs, r.page)
        if err != nil {
                r.msg = err.Error()
        }
        r.forWidth = r.textWidth()
        if path.Ext(r.page) == ".md" {
                r.rendered = renderMarkdown(src, r.forWidth)
        } else {
                r.rendered = renderText(src, r.forWidth)
        }
        for i, l := range r.rendered.links {
                _, _, ok := r.resolve(l.target)
                r.rendered.links[i].broken = !ok
        }
        r.scrollTo(r.top)
        r.moveTo(r.cursor)
}

// show shows page from line top, the cursor on line cursor.
func (r *Readme) show(page string, top, cursor int) {
        r.page, r.forWidth = page, 0
        r.render()
        r.scrollTo(top)
        r.moveTo(cursor)
}

// scrollTo scrolls to line top, kept where lines fill the screen.
func (r *Readme) scrollTo(top int) {
        r.top = max(min(top, len(r.rendered.lines)-r.bodyHeight()), 0)
}

// moveTo moves the cursor to line i, kept in the page, and scrolls it into
// view; the first link on the line that resolves is selected.
func (r *Readme) moveTo(i int) {
        n := len(r.rendered.lines)
        r.cursor = max(min(i, n-1), 0)
        r.top = scrollWindow(r.cursor, r.top, r.bodyHeight(), n)
        r.link = -1
        if r.cursor < n {
                for _, s := range r.rendered.lines[r.cursor].segs {
                        if r.live(s.link) {
                                r.link = s.link
                                break
                        }
                }
        }
}

func (r *Readme) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        r.msg = ""
        h := r.cfg.Keys.Key(keys.DocView, k)
        page := max(r.bodyHeight()-1, 1)
        if delta, paging, ok := motion(h, page, len(r.rendered.lines)); ok {
                if paging {
                        r.scrollTo(r.top + delta)
                }
                r.moveTo(r.cursor + delta)
                return nil
        }
        switch h.Action {
        case "quit":
                return tea.Quit
        case "half_page":
                delta := halfPage(h, page)
                r.scrollTo(r.top + delta)
                r.moveTo(r.cursor + delta)
        case "link":
                r.nextLink(direction(h.Index == 0))
        case "line_link":
                r.lineLink(direction(h.Index == 1))
        case "follow":
                r.follow()
        case "back":
                if len(r.back) == 0 {
                        r.msg = "no link followed to go back from"
                        break
                }
                p := r.back[len(r.back)-1]
                r.back = r.back[:len(r.back)-1]
                r.show(p.page, p.top, p.cursor)
                r.link = p.link
        case "next_page":
                i := slices.Index(r.order, r.page)
                next := i + direction(h.Index == 0)
                switch {
                case i < 0:
                        r.msg = r.page + " is no page of " + r.cfg.Keys.Text("{next_page}") + ": " + r.cfg.Keys.Text("{back}") + " goes back"
                case next < 0 || next >= len(r.order):
                        r.msg = ends(h.Index == 0, "last page", "first page")
                default:
                        r.show(r.order[next], 0, 0)
                }
        case "search":
                return askSearch
        case "match":
                r.findText(direction(h.Index == 0))
        }
        return nil
}

// live reports whether link i of the page is one: it resolves.
func (r *Readme) live(i int) bool { return i >= 0 && !r.rendered.links[i].broken }

// nextLink moves the cursor to the next link in direction dir and selects
// it: the one after the selected link, also on the same line, or the first
// below or last above the cursor.
func (r *Readme) nextLink(dir int) {
        links := r.rendered.links
        next := -1
        for i := range links {
                j := i
                if dir < 0 {
                        j = len(links) - 1 - i
                }
                after := r.link >= 0 && (j-r.link)*dir > 0 || r.link < 0 && (links[j].line-r.cursor)*dir > 0
                if after && r.live(j) {
                        next = j
                        break
                }
        }
        if next < 0 {
                r.msg = ends(dir > 0, "no link further down", "no link further up")
                return
        }
        r.moveTo(links[next].line)
        r.link = next
}

// lineLink selects the next link on the cursor's line in direction dir,
// right or left.
func (r *Readme) lineLink(dir int) {
        var on []int
        for _, s := range r.rendered.lines[min(r.cursor, len(r.rendered.lines)-1)].segs {
                if r.live(s.link) && !slices.Contains(on, s.link) {
                        on = append(on, s.link)
                }
        }
        i := slices.Index(on, r.link) + dir
        switch {
        case len(on) == 0:
                r.msg = "no link on this line"
        case i < 0 || i >= len(on):
                r.msg = ends(dir > 0, "no link further right", "no link further left")
        default:
                r.link = on[i]
        }
}

// follow follows the selected link to its page and heading, which shows at
// the top; for a web address the box of frame shows it already.
func (r *Readme) follow() {
        if r.link < 0 {
                r.msg = "no link on this line: " + r.cfg.Keys.Text("{link}") + " go to the next, previous"
                return
        }
        target := r.rendered.links[r.link].target
        page, anchor, _ := r.resolve(target)
        if page == "" {
                return
        }
        r.back = append(r.back, docPlace{r.page, r.top, r.cursor, r.link})
        r.show(page, 0, 0)
        if line := r.rendered.anchors[anchor]; anchor != "" {
                r.show(page, line, line)
        }
}

// resolve returns the file of docs and the heading that target, a link of
// the page shown, goes to, and whether both are there; for a web address
// the file is "".
func (r *Readme) resolve(target string) (page, anchor string, ok bool) {
        file, anchor, _ := strings.Cut(target, "#")
        if strings.Contains(file, ":") { // https:, mailto:
                return "", "", true
        }
        page = r.page
        if file != "" {
                page = path.Join(path.Dir(r.page), file)
                if _, err := fs.Stat(r.docs, page); err != nil {
                        return page, anchor, false
                }
        }
        if anchor == "" {
                return page, "", true
        }
        _, ok = r.anchorsOf(page)[anchor]
        return page, anchor, ok
}

// anchorsOf returns the headings of page by the names links give them, with
// their lines for the width of the page shown when it is that page.
func (r *Readme) anchorsOf(page string) map[string]int {
        if page == r.page {
                return r.rendered.anchors
        }
        a, ok := r.anchors[page]
        if !ok {
                if src, err := fs.ReadFile(r.docs, page); err == nil && path.Ext(page) == ".md" {
                        a = renderMarkdown(src, 80).anchors
                }
                r.anchors[page] = a
        }
        return a
}

// findText moves the cursor to the next line, in direction dir, that holds
// what config.Search searched for, past the cursor's own line.
func (r *Readme) findText(dir int) {
        q := r.cfg.Search.Query
        m := matcherOf(q)
        if m == nil {
                r.msg = nothingSearched
                return
        }
        var hits []int
        for i, l := range r.rendered.lines {
                if len(m.spans(l.plain())) > 0 {
                        hits = append(hits, i)
                }
        }
        if len(hits) == 0 {
                r.msg = noMatch(q, "this page")
                return
        }
        i, ok := nextHit(len(hits), dir, func(i int) int { return cmp.Compare(hits[i], r.cursor) })
        if !ok {
                r.msg = noFurther(q, dir, "")
                return
        }
        r.moveTo(hits[i])
        r.msg = searchMsg(q, i, len(hits))
}

func (r *Readme) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(r.frame()) }

func (r *Readme) frame() string {
        if r.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }
        t := r.cfg.Theme
        lines := r.rendered.lines
        var b strings.Builder
        var where []string
        if i := slices.Index(r.order, r.page); i >= 0 {
                where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("page %d/%d", i+1, len(r.order)))
        }
        if n := len(lines); n > r.bodyHeight() {
                where = append(where, fmt.Sprintf("%d%%", 100*min(r.top+r.bodyHeight(), n)/n))
        }
        b.WriteString(statusLine(t, r.width, t.Title.Render(" "+printable(r.page)), strings.Join(where, " · ")+" ") + "\n")
        m := matcherOf(r.cfg.Search.Query)
        for i := r.top; i < r.top+r.bodyHeight(); i++ {
                if i < len(lines) {
                        margin := " "
                        if i == r.cursor {
                                margin = t.Cursor.Render(margin)
                        }
                        b.WriteString(margin + r.drawLine(lines[i], m))
                }
                b.WriteString("\n")
        }
        keys := statusKeys(r.cfg, barKeys{enter: "follow", back: "back", text: true})
        b.WriteString(statusLine(t, r.width, pageStatus(r.cfg, r.msg, keys), ""))
        return r.withWebAddress(b.String())
}

// withWebAddress returns frame with a box of the web address of the selected
// link below its line, or above it near the bottom, while the cursor is on
// it; links into the documentation need none.
func (r *Readme) withWebAddress(frame string) string {
        if r.link < 0 {
                return frame
        }
        target := r.rendered.links[r.link].target
        if page, _, _ := r.resolve(target); page != "" {
                return frame
        }
        row := 1 + r.cursor - r.top // in frame, below the title
        y := row + 1
        if y+minBoxHeight > 1+r.bodyHeight() {
                y = row - minBoxHeight
        }
        if y < 1 {
                return frame // no room
        }
        x := 1 // the cursor's column, then the line up to the link
        for _, s := range r.rendered.lines[r.cursor].segs {
                if s.link == r.link {
                        break
                }
                x += ansi.StringWidth(s.text)
        }
        address := printable(target)
        w := min(ansi.StringWidth(address)+4, r.width)
        return overlayAt(frame, box(r.cfg.Theme, w, minBoxHeight, "", []string{address}), max(min(x, r.width-w), 0), y, w, r.width)
}

// drawLine draws l in the styles of its pieces, the selected link in the
// cursor style and what m finds in the search style.
func (r *Readme) drawLine(l docLine, m *matcher) string {
        var found []bool
        if m != nil {
                plain := l.plain()
                found = make([]bool, len(plain))
                for _, s := range m.spans(plain) {
                        for j := s.Start; j < s.End; j++ {
                                found[j] = true
                        }
                }
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        off := 0
        for _, s := range l.segs {
                st := r.style(s.style)
                switch {
                case s.link >= 0 && s.link == r.link:
                        st = r.cfg.Theme.Cursor
                case s.link >= 0 && !r.live(s.link):
                        st = r.style(docPlain) // leads nowhere
                }
                // Runs of the piece in or out of a match.
                for start := 0; start < len(s.text); {
                        end := start + 1
                        in := found != nil && found[off+start]
                        for end < len(s.text) && (found != nil && found[off+end]) == in {
                                end++
                        }
                        if in {
                                b.WriteString(r.cfg.Theme.Search.Render(s.text[start:end]))
                        } else {
                                b.WriteString(st.Render(s.text[start:end]))
                        }
                        start = end
                }
                off += len(s.text)
        }
        return b.String()
}

// style returns the style of the theme for a piece of a page.
func (r *Readme) style(s docStyle) lipgloss.Style {
        t := r.cfg.Theme
        switch s {
        case docBold:
                return lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)
        case docItalic:
                return lipgloss.NewStyle().Italic(true)
        case docCode:
                return t.DocCode
        case docHeading:
                return t.DocHeading
        case docLink:
                return t.DocLink
        case docDecoration:
                return t.Decoration
        case docOverflow:
                return t.Overflow
        }
        return lipgloss.NewStyle()
}

package ui

import (
        "cmp"
        "fmt"
        "regexp"
        "slices"
        "strings"
        "sync"
        "unicode"
        "unicode/utf8"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/diff"
        "jdiff/internal/settings"
)

// askSearchMsg asks SearchBar for the query to search for.
type askSearchMsg struct{}

func askSearch() tea.Msg { return askSearchMsg{} }

// findMsg asks the view shown to search for the query of config.Search from
// its cursor on, forward (1) or backward (-1).
type findMsg int

// matcher finds a query in lines. Case is ignored unless the query holds
// an upper case letter, also in a regular expression.
type matcher struct {
        q  config.Query
        re *regexp.Regexp // for a regular expression
}

// compile returns the matcher of q, or why its regular expression does not
// compile.
func compile(q config.Query) (*matcher, error) {
        m := &matcher{q: q}
        if q.Regex {
                src := q.Text
                if !hasUpper(src, true) {
                        src = "(?i)" + src
                }
                re, err := regexp.Compile(src)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                m.re = re
        }
        return m, nil
}

// compiled is the matcher used last: lines are drawn one at a time, all
// with the same query.
var compiled struct {
        sync.Mutex
        q config.Query
        m *matcher
}

// matcherOf returns the matcher of q, nil when q is empty or its regular
// expression does not compile.
func matcherOf(q config.Query) *matcher {
        if q.Text == "" {
                return nil
        }
        compiled.Lock()
        defer compiled.Unlock()
        if compiled.m == nil || compiled.q != q {
                compiled.q = q
                compiled.m, _ = compile(q)
        }
        return compiled.m
}

// spans returns where m's query is found in text, without overlaps; empty
// matches of a regular expression are left out.
func (m *matcher) spans(text string) []diff.Span {
        var out []diff.Span
        if m.re != nil {
                for _, loc := range m.re.FindAllStringIndex(text, -1) {
                        if loc[1] > loc[0] {
                                out = append(out, diff.Span{Start: loc[0], End: loc[1]})
                        }
                }
                return out
        }
        q := m.q.Text
        exact := hasUpper(q, false)
        for i := 0; i < len(text); {
                end := -1
                switch {
                case exact && strings.HasPrefix(text[i:], q):
                        end = i + len(q)
                case !exact:
                        if n := foldPrefix(text[i:], q); n >= 0 {
                                end = i + n
                        }
                }
                if end > i {
                        out = append(out, diff.Span{Start: i, End: end})
                        i = end
                        continue
                }
                _, n := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(text[i:])
                i += n
        }
        return out
}

// foldPrefix returns the length in bytes of the start of s that is q with
// case ignored, rune by rune, or -1. The lengths may differ, as of K and the
// Kelvin sign.
func foldPrefix(s, q string) int {
        j := 0
        for _, qr := range q {
                if j >= len(s) {
                        return -1
                }
                r, n := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s[j:])
                if r != qr && !strings.EqualFold(string(r), string(qr)) {
                        return -1
                }
                j += n
        }
        return j
}

// hasUpper reports whether query holds an upper case letter, which makes the
// search match case. In a regular expression the letters of escapes, as \S,
// \pL, \p{Lu} or \x4A, do not count.
func hasUpper(query string, regex bool) bool {
        if !regex {
                return strings.ContainsFunc(query, unicode.IsUpper)
        }
        for i := 0; i < len(query); {
                r, n := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(query[i:])
                i += n
                switch {
                case r == '\\' && i < len(query):
                        e, m := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(query[i:])
                        i += m
                        if e == 'p' || e == 'P' || e == 'x' {
                                switch {
                                case i < len(query) && query[i] == '{':
                                        if j := strings.IndexByte(query[i:], '}'); j >= 0 {
                                                i += j + 1
                                        }
                                case e == 'x':
                                        i = min(i+2, len(query))
                                case i < len(query):
                                        _, m := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(query[i:])
                                        i += m
                                }
                        }
                case unicode.IsUpper(r):
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// hit is a line found by /: side's line of diff row row.
type hit struct {
        row  int
        side side
}

// order is where h stands among the lines of its diff: by row, the left
// line first.
func (h hit) order() int { return 2*h.row + int(h.side) }

// hits returns the lines of a that m finds something in, in order: only
// the changed ones unless all is set.
func (a *fileAlign) hits(m *matcher, all bool) []hit {
        var out []hit
        for i, r := range a.rows {
                if !all && r.Kind == diff.Equal {
                        continue
                }
                for _, s := range []side{left, right} {
                        if line := lineOf(r, s); line >= 0 && m.spans(a.lines[s][line]) != nil {
                                out = append(out, hit{i, s})
                        }
                }
        }
        return out
}

// nextHit returns the index of the hit to go to in direction dir among n
// hits in order, which compare compares to the cursor: forward the first
// after it, backward the last before it, so that the cursor's own line is
// passed; false past the last or before the first, as the search does not
// wrap around.
func nextHit(n, dir int, compare func(i int) int) (int, bool) {
        before := func(i int) bool {
                if dir > 0 {
                        return compare(i) <= 0
                }
                return compare(i) < 0
        }
        after := 0 // the first hit not before the cursor
        for after < n && before(after) {
                after++
        }
        if dir > 0 {
                return after, after < n
        }
        return after - 1, after > 0
}

// showMatch returns the horizontal scroll that shows the first match of m
// in the line of side s of a, in a view viewWidth columns wide, scrolled by
// xoff: xoff when lines are wrapped or the match is in view, else in steps of
// 8 columns, as h and l scroll, the match a step from the left edge.
func (a *fileAlign) showMatch(m *matcher, s side, line, xoff, viewWidth int) int {
        spans := m.spans(a.lines[s][line])
        if a.cfg.Settings.Wrap || len(spans) == 0 {
                return xoff
        }
        width := a.contentWidth(viewWidth, !a.cfg.Settings.SideBySide)
        text := a.lines[s][line]
        from := ansi.StringWidth(printable(text[:spans[0].Start]))
        to := from + ansi.StringWidth(printable(text[spans[0].Start:spans[0].End]))
        if from >= xoff && to <= xoff+width-1 { // the last column may hold »
                return xoff
        }
        return max(from-8, 0) / 8 * 8
}

// nothingSearched is the message of . and , before the first search.
const nothingSearched = "nothing searched yet: / searches"

// searchMsg returns the status message of match i of n for q.
func searchMsg(q config.Query, i, n int) string {
        return queryName(q) + fmt.Sprintf(": match %d/%d · . next · , previous", i+1, n)
}

// queryName returns q as the status line names it.
func queryName(q config.Query) string {
        if q.Regex {
                return "regex /" + q.Text
        }
        return "/" + q.Text
}

// noFurther returns the status message when no match of q lies further in
// direction dir: the cursor stays.
func noFurther(q config.Query, dir int, where string) string {
        return queryName(q) + ": " + ends(dir > 0, "no match further down", "no match further up") + strings.Replace(where, " of ", " in ", 1)
}

// noMatch returns the status message when q is found nowhere in where.
func noMatch(q config.Query, where string) string {
        return queryName(q) + ": no match in " + where
}

// scope returns where / searches, as messages name it.
func scope(st settings.Settings) string {
        if st.SearchAll {
                return "all lines"
        }
        return "changed lines"
}

// findText moves the cursor to the next line, in direction dir, that holds
// what config.Search searched for, past the cursor's own line; past the
// last or first match it stays. Without wrapping lines, it scrolls the
// match into view.
func (m *FileDiff) findText(dir int) {
        q := m.cfg.Search.Query
        mt := matcherOf(q)
        if mt == nil {
                m.msg = nothingSearched
                return
        }
        hits := m.hits(mt, m.cfg.Settings.SearchAll)
        if len(hits) == 0 {
                m.msg = noMatch(q, scope(m.cfg.Settings))
                return
        }
        m.syncLayout()
        pos := hit{0, m.active}
        if m.cursor < len(m.view) {
                pos.row = m.view[m.cursor].row
        }
        i, ok := nextHit(len(hits), dir, func(i int) int { return cmp.Compare(hits[i].order(), pos.order()) })
        if !ok {
                m.msg = noFurther(q, dir, "")
                return
        }
        h := hits[i]
        line := lineOf(m.rows[h.row], h.side)
        m.jumpTo(m.reveal(h.side, line, m.shape()))
        m.xoff = m.showMatch(mt, h.side, line, m.xoff, m.width)
        m.msg = searchMsg(q, i, len(hits))
}

// flowHit is a line found by / in the flow view: hit in file of part.
type flowHit struct {
        part, file int
        hit
}

// findText moves the cursor to the next line, in direction dir, that holds
// what config.Search searched for, past the cursor's own line, in all
// files; in the log in those of the cursor's commit, N and P go to the
// others. Past the last or first match it stays. Without wrapping lines, it
// scrolls the match into view.
func (f *Flow) findText(dir int) {
        q := f.cfg.Search.Query
        mt := matcherOf(q)
        if mt == nil {
                f.msg = nothingSearched
                return
        }
        cur := 0 // the cursor's part
        if f.cursor < len(f.rows) {
                cur = f.rows[f.cursor].part
        }
        if f.inLog() && f.unloaded(cur) {
                f.await(cur, func() { f.findText(dir) })
                return
        }
        where := ""
        if f.inLog() {
                where = " of this commit"
        }
        var hits []flowHit
        for p, part := range f.parts {
                if part.diff == nil || f.inLog() && p != cur {
                        continue
                }
                for i, e := range part.diff.files {
                        if e.Binary {
                                continue
                        }
                        for _, h := range part.diff.diffOf(e).hits(mt, f.cfg.Settings.SearchAll) {
                                hits = append(hits, flowHit{p, i, h})
                        }
                }
        }
        if len(hits) == 0 {
                f.msg = noMatch(q, scope(f.cfg.Settings)+where)
                return
        }
        // The cursor's position: a commit's or file's own rows come before the
        // lines of its files.
        pos := flowHit{hit: hit{-1, f.active}}
        if f.cursor < len(f.rows) {
                r := f.rows[f.cursor]
                pos.part, pos.file = r.part, r.file
                if r.kind == flowDiff {
                        pos.row = f.parts[r.part].diff.files[r.file].align.view[r.line].row
                }
        }
        i, ok := nextHit(len(hits), dir, func(i int) int {
                a := hits[i]
                return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(a.part, pos.part), cmp.Compare(a.file, pos.file), cmp.Compare(a.order(), pos.order()))
        })
        if !ok {
                f.msg = noFurther(q, dir, where)
                return
        }
        h := hits[i]
        a := f.parts[h.part].diff.files[h.file].align
        line := lineOf(a.rows[h.row], h.side)
        at := a.reveal(h.side, line, f.built)
        f.relocate() // a fold may have opened: the cursor keeps its row
        f.jumpTo(f.findDiff(h.part, h.file, at.v, at.sub))
        f.active, f.msg = h.side, searchMsg(q, i, len(hits))
        f.xoff = a.showMatch(mt, h.side, line, f.xoff, f.width)
}

// SearchBar wraps the view: / opens a bar at the bottom to type what to
// search for, in changed lines or, switched with tab, in all, plain text
// or, switched with shift+tab, a regular expression; both are toggles,
// kept in state.toml. Up and down go through what was searched for
// before, enter searches from the cursor on, as . does. What was searched
// for is kept in config.Search.
type SearchBar struct {
        tea.Model
        cfg   *config.Config
        input *lineInput // while the bar is open
        regex bool       // the text is a regular expression, as last searched or toggled
        // hist is the entry of config.Search.History shown, its length for the
        // text typed, draft, kept while going through the history.
        hist  int
        draft config.Query
        err   string // why the regular expression does not compile, or the state is not saved

        width, height int
}

// WithSearch returns m with the search bar.
func WithSearch(cfg *config.Config, m tea.Model) *SearchBar { return &SearchBar{Model: m, cfg: cfg} }

func (s *SearchBar) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                s.width, s.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
        case askSearchMsg:
                s.input, s.regex, s.err = &lineInput{}, s.cfg.Settings.SearchRegex, ""
                s.hist = len(s.cfg.Search.History)
                return s, nil
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                if s.input != nil {
                        return s, s.key(msg)
                }
        case tea.PasteMsg:
                if s.input != nil {
                        s.err = ""
                        s.input.paste(msg.Content)
                        return s, nil
                }
        }
        var cmd tea.Cmd
        s.Model, cmd = s.Model.Update(msg)
        return s, cmd
}

// key edits the query: enter searches it, esc, enter on nothing typed or
// backspace on it closes the bar; tab switches between changed and all
// lines, shift+tab between plain text and regular expression; up and
// down go through the history, each query in its mode.
func (s *SearchBar) key(k tea.KeyPressMsg) tea.Cmd {
        in, history, st := s.input, s.cfg.Search.History, &s.cfg.Settings
        s.err = ""
        show := func(q config.Query) { in.text, s.regex = q.Text, q.Regex }
        switch {
        case k.String() == "ctrl+c":
                return tea.Quit
        case in.cancels(k):
                s.input = nil
                return nil
        }
        switch k.String() {
        case "enter":
                q := config.Query{Text: in.text, Regex: s.regex}
                if q.Text == "" {
                        s.input = nil
                        return nil
                }
                if _, err := compile(q); err != nil {
                        s.err = err.Error()
                        return nil
                }
                s.input = nil
                s.cfg.Search.Add(q)
                if st.SearchRegex != q.Regex { // from the history
                        st.SearchRegex = q.Regex
                        s.err = saveState(s.cfg)
                }
                return func() tea.Msg { return findMsg(1) }
        case "tab":
                s.err = toggle(s.cfg, &st.SearchAll)
        case "shift+tab":
                s.regex = !s.regex
                st.SearchRegex = s.regex
                s.err = saveState(s.cfg)
        case "up":
                if s.hist == len(history) {
                        s.draft = config.Query{Text: in.text, Regex: s.regex}
                }
                if s.hist > 0 {
                        s.hist--
                        show(history[s.hist])
                }
        case "down":
                if s.hist < len(history) {
                        s.hist++
                        if s.hist == len(history) {
                                show(s.draft)
                        } else {
                                show(history[s.hist])
                        }
                }
        default:
                in.edit(k)
        }
        return nil
}

// frame draws the bar over the view, above its status line, while it is
// open: the query, and why its regular expression does not compile, see
// inputBox.
func (s *SearchBar) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(s.frame()) }

func (s *SearchBar) frame() string {
        base := s.Model.View().Content
        if s.input == nil || s.width == 0 {
                return base
        }
        t := s.cfg.Theme
        // The title says how the bar searches and what tab and shift+tab switch
        // to.
        mode, otherMode := "search", "regex"
        if s.regex {
                mode, otherMode = "regex", "text"
        }
        otherScope := "all"
        if s.cfg.Settings.SearchAll {
                otherScope = "changed"
        }
        keys := []string{"enter find", "tab " + otherScope, "shift+tab " + otherMode, "esc cancel"}
        if len(s.cfg.Search.History) > 0 {
                keys = slices.Insert(keys, 1, "↑↓ history")
        }
        title := popupTitle(t, mode+" "+scope(s.cfg.Settings), "", strings.Join(keys, " · "))
        bar, w := inputBox(t, s.width, title, "/"+printable(s.input.text), s.err, false)
        // Above the status line, the last row.
        y := max(strings.Count(base, "\n")-strings.Count(bar, "\n"), 0)
        return overlayAt(base, bar, max((s.width-w)/2, 0), y, w, s.width)
}

package ui

import (
        "cmp"
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/config"
        "jdiff/internal/keys"
)

// settingOption is a line of the settings popup: the key that toggles it
// there and on the pages, the action of its row in keybindings.toml, what it
// does while on, from that row, and the toggle. With ask, the page below
// toggles it, when it can. With logFlow, its key works only in the log's flow
// view, where it shows; in the popup always. With number instead of the toggle,
// it is a number, asked for in a line; it has no key, the cursor goes to it in
// the popup.
type settingOption struct {
        key, action, what string
        setting           func(*config.Config) *bool
        ask               tea.Cmd
        logFlow           bool
        number            func(*config.Config) *int
}

// settingTable lists the settings without their keys and words, which
// settingOptions takes from the keys.
var settingTable = []settingOption{
        {action: "layout", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.SideBySide }},
        {action: "flow", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.Flow }, ask: toggleFlow},
        {action: "untracked", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.Untracked }, ask: toggleUntracked},
        {action: "wrap", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.Wrap }},
        {action: "whitespace", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.ShowWhitespaces }},
        {action: "mark_whitespace", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.MarkWhitespace }},
        {action: "trailing", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.ShowTrailing }},
        {action: "line_ends", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.ShowCR }},
        {action: "bold", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.BoldChanges }},
        {action: "preview", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.Preview }},
        {action: "full_messages", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.FullMessages }, logFlow: true},
        {action: "stay_in_commit", setting: func(c *config.Config) *bool { return &c.Settings.StayInCommit }, logFlow: true},
        {action: "auto_reload_timeout", number: func(c *config.Config) *int { return &c.Settings.AutoReloadTimeout }},
        {action: "inotify_max_dirs", number: func(c *config.Config) *int { return &c.Settings.InotifyMaxDirs }},
}

// settingOptions returns the lines of the settings popup, with their keys and
// what they do from km, in its order.
func settingOptions(km *keys.Map) []settingOption {
        options, _ := orderSettings(km.Order(), settingWords(km, settingTable), nil)
        return options
}

// helpLines returns the lines of the help popup, from km, the settings in its
// order.
func helpLines(km *keys.Map) []binding {
        _, bs := orderSettings(km.Order(), settingWords(km, settingTable), helpBindings(km))
        return bs
}

// settingWords returns options with their keys and what they do from km.
func settingWords(km *keys.Map, options []settingOption) []settingOption {
        options = slices.Clone(options)
        for i := range options {
                r := km.Row(options[i].action)
                if len(r.Keys) > 0 {
                        options[i].key = r.Keys[0]
                }
                options[i].what = km.Text(r.Setting)
        }
        return options
}

// isAction reports whether key k does action on all pages of km: the popups
// over them open and close on such keys.
func isAction(km *keys.Map, k, action string) bool {
        return km.Key(keys.Everywhere, k).Action == action
}

// orderSettings returns options and bindings with the settings in the order of
// the keys order. Options not in it, as c and C, which keybindings.toml lists
// with the flow view, stay behind, as they are; the other bindings stay where
// they are.
func orderSettings(order []string, options []settingOption, bs []binding) ([]settingOption, []binding) {
        rank := func(k string) int {
                if i := slices.Index(order, k); i >= 0 {
                        return i
                }
                return len(order)
        }
        options = slices.Clone(options)
        slices.SortStableFunc(options, func(a, b settingOption) int { return cmp.Compare(rank(a.key), rank(b.key)) })

        isSetting := func(b binding) bool {
                return b.key != "" && slices.Contains(order, b.key) && slices.ContainsFunc(options, func(o settingOption) bool { return o.key == b.key })
        }
        bs = slices.Clone(bs)
        var at []int
        var settings []binding
        for i, b := range bs {
                if isSetting(b) {
                        at, settings = append(at, i), append(settings, b)
                }
        }
        slices.SortStableFunc(settings, func(a, b binding) int { return cmp.Compare(rank(a.key), rank(b.key)) })
        for i, b := range settings {
                bs[at[i]] = b
        }
        return options, bs
}

// keyOrderProblems returns what is wrong with text, a keybindings.toml, for the
// settings and the help of the built-in keys: a row of all pages with a key
// that does not exist or stands twice, a setting missing or without words, and
// the problems of its keys by themselves (keys.Map.Problems).
func keyOrderProblems(text string) []string {
        p, err := keys.Parse(text)
        if err != nil {
                return []string{err.Error()}
        }
        var problems []string
        order := p.Order()
        if len(order) == 0 {
                return []string{"no rows in everywhere"}
        }
        options, bindings := settingOptions(keys.Builtin()), helpLines(keys.Builtin())
        for i, k := range order {
                if slices.Contains(order[:i], k) {
                        problems = append(problems, "key "+k+" stands twice")
                }
                if !slices.ContainsFunc(bindings, func(b binding) bool { return slices.Contains(strings.Fields(b.key), k) }) {
                        problems = append(problems, "key "+k+" is not in the help")
                }
        }
        for _, o := range options {
                if o.key != "" && !o.logFlow && !slices.Contains(order, o.key) {
                        problems = append(problems, "setting "+o.key+" is not in the table")
                }
        }
        for _, o := range options {
                if p.Row(o.action).Setting == "" {
                        problems = append(problems, "setting "+o.action+" has no words")
                }
        }
        return append(problems, p.Problems()...)
}

// SettingsPopup wraps the top-level model and shows the display toggles in a
// popup over it on askSettingsMsg. It stays open after a toggle, so that
// several can be set while the page below shows them.
type SettingsPopup struct {
        tea.Model
        cfg     *config.Config
        options []settingOption // of cfg.Keys, which do not change
        open    bool
        listPopup
        msg      string     // a problem saving the toggles
        input    *lineInput // while it asks for the number of the option
        inputErr string     // why the number typed is none
}

// WithSettings returns m with the settings popup.
func WithSettings(cfg *config.Config, m tea.Model) *SettingsPopup {
        return &SettingsPopup{Model: m, cfg: cfg, options: settingOptions(cfg.Keys)}
}

func (p *SettingsPopup) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
        switch msg := msg.(type) {
        case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
                p.width, p.height = msg.Width, msg.Height
                p.move(0)
        case askSettingsMsg:
                p.open, p.cursor, p.top, p.msg = true, 0, 0, ""
                return p, nil
        case toggleSettingMsg:
                i := slices.IndexFunc(p.options, func(o settingOption) bool { return o.action == msg.action })
                return p, p.flip(p.options[i])
        case tea.KeyPressMsg:
                switch {
                case p.input != nil:
                        return p, p.editNumber(msg)
                case p.open:
                        return p, p.key(msg.String())
                }
        case tea.PasteMsg:
                if p.input != nil {
                        p.inputErr = ""
                        p.input.paste(msg.Content)
                        return p, nil
                }
        }
        _, cmd := p.Model.Update(msg)
        return p, cmd
}

// key handles k in the open popup.
func (p *SettingsPopup) key(k string) tea.Cmd {
        if cmd, ok := popupNav(p.cfg.Keys, k, "settings", &p.open, p.move); ok {
                return cmd
        }
        switch k {
        case "enter", "space":
                return p.flip(p.options[p.cursor])
        default:
                if i := slices.IndexFunc(p.options, func(o settingOption) bool { return o.key != "" && keys.Name(o.key) == k }); i >= 0 {
                        p.move(i - p.cursor)
                        return p.flip(p.options[i])
                }
        }
        return nil
}

// flip toggles option o and tells the page below; a problem saving it shows
// in the popup while that is open, else on the page.
func (p *SettingsPopup) flip(o settingOption) tea.Cmd {
        if o.number != nil {
                p.input, p.inputErr = &lineInput{text: strconv.Itoa(*o.number(p.cfg))}, ""
                return nil
        }
        if o.ask != nil {
                return o.ask
        }
        problem := toggle(p.cfg, o.setting(p.cfg))
        if p.open {
                p.msg, problem = problem, ""
        }
        _, cmd := p.Model.Update(settingChangedMsg{o.action, problem})
        return cmd
}

// editNumber edits the number asked for: enter sets and saves it, unless it is
// none, esc and backspace on nothing typed cancel.
func (p *SettingsPopup) editNumber(k tea.KeyPressMsg) tea.Cmd {
        in := p.input
        switch {
        case k.String() == "ctrl+c":
                return tea.Quit
        case in.cancels(k):
                p.input = nil
        case k.String() == "enter":
                n, err := strconv.Atoi(strings.TrimSpace(in.text))
                if err != nil || n < 0 {
                        p.inputErr = "a whole number, 0 or more"
                        return nil
                }
                o := p.options[p.cursor]
                *o.number(p.cfg) = n
                p.input, p.msg = nil, saveState(p.cfg)
                _, cmd := p.Model.Update(settingChangedMsg{action: o.action})
                return cmd
        default:
                p.inputErr = ""
                in.edit(k)
        }
        return nil
}

func (p *SettingsPopup) move(delta int) { p.moveIn(delta, len(p.options)) }

func (p *SettingsPopup) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(p.frame()) }

func (p *SettingsPopup) frame() string {
        base := p.Model.View().Content
        if !p.open || p.width == 0 {
                return base
        }
        t := p.cfg.Theme
        options := p.options
        n := len(options)
        all := make([]string, n)
        states := make([]string, n)
        keyW, stateW := 0, 0
        for i, o := range options {
                switch {
                case o.number != nil:
                        states[i] = fmt.Sprintf("[%d]", *o.number(p.cfg))
                case *o.setting(p.cfg):
                        states[i] = "[x]"
                default:
                        states[i] = "[ ]"
                }
                keyW = max(keyW, ansi.StringWidth(o.key))
                stateW = max(stateW, ansi.StringWidth(states[i]))
        }
        for i, o := range options {
                all[i] = fmt.Sprintf(" %-*s %-*s  %s ", stateW, states[i], keyW, o.key, o.what)
        }
        title := popupTitle(t, "settings", p.msg, "enter toggle · esc close")
        view := p.draw(t, base, title, all, "")
        if p.input == nil {
                return view
        }
        pop, popW := inputBox(t, p.width, popupTitle(t, "auto reload timeout", "", "enter set · esc cancel"), printable(p.input.text), p.inputErr, true)
        return overlay(view, pop, popW, p.width)
}

package ui

import (
        "fmt"
        "slices"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "unicode"
        "unicode/utf8"

        tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
        "charm.land/lipgloss/v2"
        "github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"

        "jdiff/internal/diff"
)

const tabWidth = 4

// lineStyles are the styles of one rendered line.
type lineStyles struct {
        base  lipgloss.Style // text outside emphasized spans
        emph  lipgloss.Style // changed words
        space lipgloss.Style // changed whitespace, drawn as · and →
        ws    lipgloss.Style // other whitespace, drawn as · and → when showAll
        // showAll draws all whitespace visibly, not only changed whitespace.
        showAll bool
        // plainSpace draws changed whitespace like unchanged whitespace.
        plainSpace bool
        // showTrailing draws whitespace at the end of the line visibly in
        // trailing; with showAll the end of the line is drawn as lineEnd.
        trailing     lipgloss.Style
        showTrailing bool
        lineEnd      string
        // cr is drawn for the \r of a \r\n line end where it changed, and with
        // showCR on all lines.
        cr     string
        showCR bool
        // hidden is the style of ☠, drawn for invisible characters.
        hidden lipgloss.Style
        // matches are the parts of the line found by /, drawn in match over all
        // other styles.
        matches []diff.Span
        match   lipgloss.Style
}

// painter draws text in a style as lipgloss renders it, without the cost of
// a lipgloss render for each run of a line: the escape sequences come from
// rendering a sample once. Underlined and struck through styles are
// rendered by lipgloss, which styles their spaces apart.
type painter struct {
        style       lipgloss.Style
        open, close string
        slow        bool
}

func newPainter(st lipgloss.Style) painter {
        p := painter{style: st, slow: st.GetUnderline() || st.GetStrikethrough()}
        if !p.slow {
                out := st.Render("x")
                i := strings.Index(out, "x")
                p.open, p.close = out[:i], out[i+1:]
        }
        return p
}

func (p painter) paint(s string) string {
        if p.slow {
                return p.style.Render(s)
        }
        return p.open + s + p.close
}

// hiddenSymbol stands for characters that are not visible: control and
// format characters, among them those that change the direction of text and
// could reorder what is shown, zero-width spaces and joiners, and the line
// and paragraph separators. Spaces that take room, as the no-break space,
// are visible: they are drawn as they are.
const hiddenSymbol = "☠"

// hiddenChar reports whether r is drawn as hiddenSymbol.
func hiddenChar(r rune) bool {
        if r < utf8.RuneSelf { // ASCII: only control characters
                return r != '\t' && (r < 0x20 || r == 0x7f)
        }
        return unicode.In(r, unicode.Cc, unicode.Cf, unicode.Zl, unicode.Zp)
}

// zwj is the zero-width joiner, which joins emoji into one sign, as the man,
// woman and girl of a family.
const zwj = '\u200d'

// emojiJoiners returns the byte offsets of the joiners in s that join two
// emoji into one sign. They are not drawn, so that the sign shows as its
// emoji: terminals that cannot join emoji draw it so, wider than one sign,
// and the panes would not line up. A joiner elsewhere, as between letters,
// is invisible, a hidden character.
func emojiJoiners(s string) []int {
        var out []int
        for i, r := range s {
                if r != zwj || i == 0 {
                        continue
                }
                prev, _ := utf8.DecodeLastRuneInString(s[:i])
                next, _ := utf8.DecodeRuneInString(s[i+utf8.RuneLen(zwj):])
                // Emoji are symbols; the one before may end in the selector of its
                // emoji form or a skin tone.
                if (unicode.Is(unicode.So, prev) || prev == '\ufe0f' || prev >= 0x1f3fb && prev <= 0x1f3ff) &&
                        unicode.Is(unicode.So, next) {
                        out = append(out, i)
                }
        }
        return out
}

// dropJoiners returns text without its emoji joiners (see emojiJoiners), and
// the byte ranges spans and matches of text as ranges of the text left.
func dropJoiners(text string, spans, matches []diff.Span) (string, []diff.Span, []diff.Span) {
        at := emojiJoiners(text)
        if len(at) == 0 {
                return text, spans, matches
        }
        n := utf8.RuneLen(zwj)
        var b strings.Builder
        from := 0
        for _, i := range at {
                b.WriteString(text[from:i])
                from = i + n
        }
        b.WriteString(text[from:])
        // shift moves an offset back by the joiners before it.
        shift := func(p int) int {
                before := 0
                for _, i := range at {
                        if i < p {
                                before++
                        }
                }
                return p - before*n
        }
        moved := func(in []diff.Span) []diff.Span {
                var out []diff.Span
                for _, sp := range in {
                        out = append(out, diff.Span{Start: shift(sp.Start), End: shift(sp.End)})
                }
                return out
        }
        return b.String(), moved(spans), moved(matches)
}

// sideStyles returns the styles of side s for whole-line changes, the
// unchanged parts of changed lines, changed words and changed whitespace.
func (a *fileAlign) sideStyles(s side) (line, nonEmph, emph, indent lipgloss.Style) {
        t := a.cfg.Theme
        if s == left {
                return t.Minus, t.MinusNonEmph, t.MinusEmph, t.MinusIndent
        }
        return t.Plus, t.PlusNonEmph, t.PlusEmph, t.PlusIndent
}

// help returns the keys of the status bar: backspace and f when shown from a
// file list, N/P in the log.
func (m *FileDiff) help() string {
        k := barKeys{enter: "anchor", commits: m.commitNav, flow: m.canBack}
        if m.canBack {
                k.back = "to_files"
        }
        return statusKeys(m.cfg, k)
}

func (m *FileDiff) View() tea.View { return tea.NewView(m.frame()) }

func (m *FileDiff) frame() string {
        if m.width == 0 {
                return ""
        }
        var b strings.Builder
        b.WriteString(m.header() + "\n")
        m.syncLayout()
        pos := m.pos()
        for i := m.top; i < m.top+m.bodyHeight(); i++ {
                if i < len(m.screen) {
                        b.WriteString(m.screenLine(m.screen[i], i == pos))
                }
                b.WriteString("\n")
        }
        b.WriteString(m.status())
        return b.String()
}

func (a *fileAlign) numWidth() int {
        return len(strconv.Itoa(max(len(a.lines[left]), len(a.lines[right]), 1)))
}

// header renders the file names above both panes.
func (m *FileDiff) header() string {
        return paneTitles(m.cfg.Theme, m.names, m.active, m.cfg.Settings.SideBySide, m.width)
}

// screenLine renders screen line s in the current layout, marked as the
// cursor line when cursor is set.
func (m *FileDiff) screenLine(s sline, cursor bool) string {
        return m.render(s, cursor, layout{m.width, m.xoff, m.active, m.screenShape})
}

// render renders screen line s in layout lo, marked as the cursor line when
// cursor is set.
func (a *fileAlign) render(s sline, cursor bool, lo layout) string {
        if lo.unified {
                return a.unifiedLine(s.v, s.sub, cursor, lo)
        }
        return a.bodyRow(s.v, s.sub, cursor, lo)
}

// startCol returns the first column of a line shown in a pane width columns
// wide: of its piece when lines are wrapped, else the scroll offset.
func startCol(piece, width int, lo layout) int {
        if lo.wrap > 0 {
                return piece * width
        }
        return lo.xoff
}

// unifiedLine renders line sub of displayed row v in the normal layout:
// marker, old and new line number, separator and the content. Unchanged
// lines show both numbers, changed ones the number of their side, further
// pieces of a wrapped line none.
func (a *fileAlign) unifiedLine(v, sub int, cursor bool, lo layout) string {
        if a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return a.fold(v, cursor, lo.width)
        }
        t := a.cfg.Theme
        row := a.rows[a.view[v].row]
        s, piece := a.unifiedPiece(v, sub, lo.shape)
        nw := a.numWidth()
        changed := row.Kind != diff.Equal
        number := func(line int, of side) string {
                if line < 0 || (changed && of != s) || piece > 0 {
                        return strings.Repeat(" ", nw)
                }
                return fmt.Sprintf("%*d", nw, line+1)
        }
        nums := number(row.Left, left) + " " + number(row.Right, right)
        pending := piece == 0 && a.isPending(row) && (row.Kind == diff.Equal || a.pending.side == s)
        line := lineOf(row, s)
        _, moved := a.movedTo(s, line)
        ls := lineState{s: s, row: row, line: line, changed: changed, pending: pending, moved: moved,
                cont: piece > 0, cursor: cursor, active: cursor}
        gutter := a.gutter(ls, nums, t.Decoration)
        width := a.contentWidth(lo.width, true)
        return gutter + a.content(ls, width, startCol(piece, width, lo), lo.wrap == 0)
}

// bodyRow renders line sub of displayed row v side by side, marked as the
// cursor row when cursor is set.
func (a *fileAlign) bodyRow(v, sub int, cursor bool, lo layout) string {
        if a.view[v].hidden > 0 {
                return a.fold(v, cursor, lo.width)
        }
        w, nw := (lo.width-1)/2, a.numWidth()
        return a.cell(left, v, sub, w, nw, cursor, lo) + a.cfg.Theme.Decoration.Render("│") +
